Compositional reasoning performance remains close to the teacher on SugarCrepe (86.1 vs. 86.4), MR2-Bench (24.5 vs. 24.7), and ARO (59.5 vs. 60.4), while Winoground group performance (6.8 vs. 8.5) remains the primary limitation. Depth pruning also reduces forward latency by approximately 10% (150.0 to 136.4 ms per image on NVIDIA DGX Spark using FlashAttention-2). We present the architecture, compression methodology, training procedures, and evaluation results, demonstrating the effectiveness of Eddy-VL for multimodal retrieval under constrained edge deployment. Model weights and inference code are publicly available on Hugging Face.

</details>


### [35] [Monte Carlo Dropout Uncertainty and Entropy-Thresholded Selective Prediction for Architecture-Agnostic Brain Tumor MRI Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16317)
*Medhansh Sharma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向四类脑肿瘤MRI分类的不确定性优先管线，通过MC Dropout的熵值设定明确延迟规则，将最不确定的5%病例交由放射科医生处理，使剩余病例准确率提升至约0.98，解决了确定性置信度塌缩问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在脑肿瘤MRI分型上已达专家水平，但模型置信度不可靠——确定性置信度头训练后输出近乎常数，无法指示分类正确性，导致模型难以在临床中安全部署。需要一种可信任的不确定性估计方法来标记可能误判的病例并延迟给人类专家。

Method: 采用不确定性优先管线，基于MC Dropout（T=20次前向传播）读取预测熵，将熵值转化为明确的延迟规则；使用感知哈希聚类分割7200张图像，消除近重复泄漏；在ViT-B/16和ResNet-50上评估，涵盖判别力、校准度和选择性预测三个维度。

Result: 两种网络判别力无显著差异（macro-AUC 0.994，准确率0.962/0.964），温度缩放使ECE降至0.016-0.020；延迟最不确定5%病例后，剩余病例准确率升至约0.98，风险-覆盖曲线下面积0.010-0.011。

Conclusion: MC Dropout不确定性在校准后不塌缩且可直接通过具体延迟规则应用，为校准的、可延迟给人类的脑肿瘤MRI分类提供了架构无关的基础。

Abstract: Deep networks now subtype brain tumors on MRI about as well as specialist readers, yet accuracy is not what keeps them out of the clinic. What matters at the point of care is whether a model's confidence can be trusted to flag the cases it is likely to misclassify and defer them to a human. Deterministic estimates cannot: an auxiliary confidence head trained alongside the classifier collapses to a near-constant output that says nothing about correctness. This study proposes an uncertainty-first pipeline for four-class brain tumor MRI (glioma, meningioma, pituitary, no tumor) that reads predictive uncertainty from Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout over T = 20 passes and turns the resulting entropy into an explicit rule for deferring uncertain cases to a radiologist. We partitioned 7,200 images by perceptual-hash cluster, closing the near-duplicate leakage that inflates accuracy under naive splitting, and evaluated the pipeline on ViT-B/16 and ResNet-50 across five seeds along three axes: discrimination, calibration, and selective prediction. Both discriminate strongly (macro-AUC 0.994; accuracy 0.962 and 0.964), and no seed separates them (0 of 5 significant, p < 0.05), so the result is driven by the uncertainty pipeline, not the network. A single temperature scalar pulls the deterministic softmax into tight calibration (expected calibration error 0.016-0.020), and deferring the most uncertain 5% of cases lifts accuracy on the rest to about 0.98 on both (area under the risk-coverage curve 0.010-0.011). MC-Dropout uncertainty here is thus calibrated, non-collapsing, and directly actionable through a concrete deferral rule, providing an architecture-agnostic basis for calibrated, defer-to-human brain tumor MRI triage under internal validation.

</details>


### [36] [Deep Learning-based Filtering for Video Coding: A Survey on Architectures, Algorithms, and Complexity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16319)
*Young-Woon Lee,Byung-Gyu Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对面向消费电子设备的深度学习滤波（DLF）技术进行了全面的硬件导向综述，提出了基于集成方案、编码信息利用和网络设计策略的三维分类法，分析了率失真性能与硬件可行性之间的权衡，并纳入JVET标准化活动，为下一代低功耗智能视频编码提供了路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高清显示和沉浸式媒体在IoT和CE领域（如8K显示、移动设备）的普及对高效视频编码提出了前所未有的需求。尽管DLF是减轻HEVC/VVC压缩伪影的有效方案，但其在CE设备上的部署受限于计算复杂度、内存带宽和功耗，迫切需要弥合学术研究与实际部署之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出系统性三维分类法，从(1)编码集成方案、(2)编码信息利用和(3)网络设计策略三个维度对DLF技术进行梳理。与以往综述不同，本文重点分析率失真性能与硬件可行性之间的权衡，追踪从重载性能导向模型向轻量化硬件友好架构（面向NPU）的演进，并纳入JVET关于NNVC的最新标准化活动。

Result: 揭示了DLF技术从高复杂度性能导向模型向轻量级硬件友好架构演进的趋势，提供了基于硬件可行性的分类与评估框架，整合了最新的JVET标准化进展。

Conclusion: 识别了实时推理延迟和误差传播等开放挑战，为下一代CE视觉端点的鲁棒、低功耗智能视频编码提供了发展路线图。

Abstract: As Ultra-High-Definition (UHD) displays and immersive media services become ubiquitous in the Internet of Things (IoT) and Consumer Electronics (CE) sectors, including 8K display and mobile devices, the demand for high-efficiency video coding is unprecedented. While Deep Learning-based Filtering (DLF) has emerged as a promising solution to mitigate compression artifacts inherent in standards like High Efficiency Video Coding (HEVC/H.265) and Versatile Video Coding (VVC/H.266), its deployment in CE devices is severely constrained by computational complexity, memory bandwidth, and power consumption. To bridge the gap between academic research and practical deployment, this paper presents a comprehensive, hardware-oriented survey of DLF techniques. We propose a systematic three-dimensional taxonomy classifying methods into (1) Integration Scheme within the Video Coding, (2) Coding Information Utilization, and (3) Network Design Strategy. Unlike prior reviews, this work critically analyzes the trade-offs between Rate-Distortion (RD) performance and hardware feasibility, highlighting the evolution from heavy, performance-oriented models to lightweight, hardware-friendly architectures targeting Neural Processing Units (NPUs). Furthermore, we incorporate the latest standardization activities from the Joint Video Experts Team (JVET) on Neural Network-based Video Coding (NNVC) to provide realistic guidelines. We also identify open challenges such as real-time inference latency and error propagation, providing a roadmap toward robust, low-power intelligent video coding in next-generation CE vision endpoints.

</details>


### [37] [The Devil is in the Dark Pixels: Toward Brightness Bias-Robust Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16320)
*Sungjun Cho,Zhuangzhuang Chen,Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了图像去噪中一个被忽视的问题：在信号依赖的相机噪声模型下，MSE训练的模型对暗区存在亮度偏差，导致暗像素重建效果差。作者提出BBRD方法，通过分亮度带归一化误差并应用Group-DRO动态加权，在不增加参数和推理成本的情况下，显著提升各亮度带的PSNR，尤其在暗区效果最为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MSE损失函数在信号依赖噪声模型下存在亮度偏差：暗区信噪比天然低，但MSE训练反而进一步恶化这一问题，导致暗像素重建比其噪声基底差6倍。根本原因在于信号依赖噪声使得亮像素残差更大，且网络雅可比范数随亮度单调增加，导致亮区主导梯度更新，暗区被忽视。

Method: 提出Brightness Bias-Robust Denoising (BBRD)，核心包括：(1) 将像素按亮度分带；(2) 各带内误差按经验噪声方差归一化；(3) 采用Group Distributionally Robust Optimization (Group-DRO)动态提升当前表现最差亮度带的权重。该方法无需额外参数，零推理成本，可作为MSE损失的即插即用替代方案。

Result: 在8种网络架构和2个数据集上，BBRD是13种对比方法中唯一能同时提升所有亮度带性能的方法。在SIDD数据集上，暗带PSNR提升最高+0.45 dB，亮带提升+0.32 dB，总体PSNR提升+0.65 dB，暗区增益最大。

Conclusion: BBRD有效解决了信号依赖噪声下图像去噪的亮度偏差问题，实现了各亮度带的均衡提升，无需额外参数和推理成本，是一种高效实用的MSE替代方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we reveal an important yet overlooked problem in image denoising: under signal-dependent camera noise models, dark regions suffer from inherently low Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR), as signal intensity decays far faster than noise variance diminishes, making detail recovery in dark areas fundamentally challenging. Yet rather than compensating for this difficulty, MSE-trained denoisers exacerbate it -- reconstructing dark pixels up to 6x worse relative to their per-band noise floor. This bias stems from two compounding factors: signal-dependent noise inflates bright-pixel residuals, and the network's Jacobian norm increases monotonically with brightness. Together, these cause bright regions to chronically dominate gradient updates at the expense of dark ones. To this end, we propose Brightness Bias-Robust Denoising (BBRD), a drop-in replacement for MSE loss that partitions pixels into brightness bands, normalizes per-band error by empirical noise variance, and applies Group Distributionally Robust Optimization (Group-DRO) to dynamically upweight whichever band is currently worst, with zero additional parameters or inference cost. Across 8 architectures and 2 datasets in our experiments, BBRD is the only method among 13 tested alternatives that improves each brightness band simultaneously, achieving up to +0.45 dB on dark bands, +0.32 dB on bright bands, and +0.65 dB aggregate Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR) on SIDD, with the largest per-band gains in the darkest regions where detail recovery matters most. Code is available at https://github.com/xmed-lab/BBRD

</details>


### [38] [Art Beyond Semantics: Sheaf-Informed Contrastive Learning for Multi-Relational Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16321)
*Ludovica Schaerf,Antonio Purificato,Piera Riccio,Fabrizio Silvestri,Noa Garcia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CANVAS框架，利用层论（sheaf theory）学习基于多重关系的视觉-语言对齐，解决CLIP等模型将艺术分析中多元语义关系压缩为单一对齐空间的问题。在三个新构建的艺术理解基准上，CANVAS在多模态检索和艺术理解任务中优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 艺术史分析需要从风格、图像学、历史语境等多个不可互换的维度理解作品，每个维度都承载着视觉与文本之间不同的语义关系。然而，现有VLMs（如CLIP）通过单一共享嵌入空间学习对齐，将这些丰富多元的关系结构压缩为同质化的单一对齐，丧失了艺术史推理所需的多关系结构。

Method: 提出CANVAS框架：1）基于层论（sheaf theory），将每件艺术作品投影到多个嵌入表示，每个嵌入由关系类型（即上下文）条件化；2）设计新型对比损失函数，在训练过程中编码上下文信息；3）推理阶段无需依赖外部数据。同时构建了三个新的多关系艺术理解基准：WikiArt+、HertzianaDP和SemArt+。

Result: 在多模态检索和艺术理解任务上，CANVAS在三个基准（WikiArt+、HertzianaDP、SemArt+）上均优于现有基线模型，证明多关系对齐不仅在理论上有依据，在实践中也是必要的。

Conclusion: 多关系对齐（multi-relational alignment）对于艺术理解至关重要，基于层论的多嵌入空间方法能够有效捕捉艺术史分析中复杂的视觉-文本语义关系，为多模态表示学习提供了新的理论视角和实践框架。

Abstract: Understanding a painting is never a single act. Art historians may analyze the same work through concepts of style, iconography, or historical context, dimensions that are not interchangeable, and each carries distinct semantic relationships between the visual and the textual. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) like CLIP, which learn a single shared embedding space, collapse this richness into a single homogeneous alignment, thereby losing the multi-relational structure that defines art-historical reasoning. We introduce CANVAS (Contrastive Art-aware Network for Vision-Language Alignment with Sheaves), a framework for learning relation-aware multimodal representations inspired by sheaf theory. Each artwork is projected into multiple embeddings conditioned on the type of relation (i.e., the context), and a novel contrastive loss encodes contextual information during training, with no dependency on external data at inference. We evaluate on three newly introduced benchmarks of artworks for multi-relational art understanding: WikiArt+, derived from WikiArt and Wikipedia, HertzianaDP, from the Bibliotheca Hertziana collection, and SemArt+, refined from the SemArt dataset. In multimodal retrieval and art understanding, CANVAS outperforms the baselines, supporting the view that multi-relational alignment is not just theoretically motivated but also practically essential.

</details>


### [39] [GMoT: Gated Motion-Aware Tokenization for Fine-Grained Micro-Gesture Video Reasoning with Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16322)
*Taorui Wang,Wei Xia,Hui Ma,Zijia Song,Jiayu Zhang,Zeheng Wang,Yong Xu,Zitong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GMoT（门控运动感知标记化模块），通过显式提取稀疏运动证据并融合到视觉流中，结合渐进式奖励引导策略优化，在微手势识别任务上超越现有方法，并引入身体区域定位召回率作为新的评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在通用视频理解上表现优异，但难以捕捉微手势中微弱的运动信号，易被静态外观和背景噪声淹没，且倾向于依赖静态姿态先验。

Method: 1) GMoT模块：通过空间加权池化动态定位动作相关区域，提取帧间时序差分捕获运动能量，利用保守初始化的语义门控自适应融合运动线索到视觉流；2) 渐进式奖励引导策略优化；3) 半监督标注管道生成解剖学聚焦的文本描述。

Result: 在iMiGUE和SMG数据集上分别达到67.32%和73.11%的Top-1准确率，相较于Qwen3-VL-8B基线分别提升+6.80和+3.11个百分点。

Conclusion: GMoT增强模型不仅在域内准确率上取得提升，在标签保持扰动下仍保持增益，在跨域迁移场景下也表现出改进，同时生成的推理文本保持较高的解剖学定位质量。

Abstract: Micro-gesture recognition demands the detection of fleeting, spatially localized movements that are frequently overwhelmed by dominant static appearances and background noise. While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel at general video understanding, they inherently struggle with subtle kinematics and often rely on static posture priors. To this end, we propose GMoT, a Gated Motion-Aware Tokenization module that explicitly distills sparse kinematic evidence into a compact sequence prior to temporal modeling. GMoT dynamically spotlights action-relevant regions via spatially weighted pooling, extracts adjacent-frame temporal differencing to capture precise motion energy, and adaptively fuses these cues into the visual stream using a conservatively initialized semantic gate. To transition from simple classification to evidence-grounded reasoning, we further introduce a progressive reward-guided policy refinement paradigm, supported by a semi-supervised annotation pipeline that generates anatomically focused captions. Beyond achieving the best Top-1 accuracy among the compared methods on iMiGUE (67.32\%) and SMG (73.11\%), improving the Qwen3-VL-8B baseline by +6.80 and +3.11 points, our framework introduces Body-Region Grounding (BRG) Recall as an anatomical-grounding proxy conditioned on correct predictions, together with an overlapping-label cross-domain transfer protocol between iMiGUE and SMG. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our GMoT-augmented model improves in-domain accuracy, retains clear gains under label-preserving corruptions, and improves accuracy-oriented cross-domain transfer under explicit small-split caveats while maintaining high anatomical grounding in its generated rationales.

</details>


### [40] [SGMCE: Segment-Grounded Morphological Concept Explanation for Malaria Parasite Species Identification in Thick Blood Smears](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16324)
*Ahmed Tahiru Issah,Charles B. Delahunt,Carine Mukamakuza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SGMCE是一个无需额外训练或形态学标注的后验解释框架，能针对厚血膜疟疾检测生成基于形态学的自然语言解释。它提取掩码引导的裁剪缩略图，计算14个手工CV形态学特征，并查询GPT-4o生成结构化解释。在737个检测样本中，KBC达0.91，DS达0.99，CCF达0.97。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习疟疾检测器仅提供分类结果，缺乏形态学证据支持，导致显微镜检查人员无法在病例层面审计模型预测。作者希望构建一个无需额外训练数据或标注的解释框架，为每项检测提供可追溯的形态学依据。

Method: SGMCE框架包含四个核心步骤：1）提取掩码引导的裁剪缩略图；2）基于自适应掩码内阈值计算14个手工CV形态学特征（形状、颜色、染色质、疟色素）；3）结合视觉证据和计算测量值查询GPT-4o，并以WHO厚血膜辅助工具编译的知识库为条件约束；4）输出结构化解释，指出支持检出物种的形态特征及排除竞争物种的原因。使用四种自动指标验证：KBC、CCF、DS和LLMj，并采用句子级语义评分规则配合物种感知的否定过滤解决词汇不匹配问题。

Result: 在包含四种疟原虫物种和白细胞的139张厚血膜图像、737个检测样本上，寄生虫类的平均KBC为0.91，平均DS为0.99，平均CCF为0.97。逐规则的CCF分解证实，视觉-语言模型提出的基于CV的主张与其引用的测量值一致。

Conclusion: SGMCE成功实现了无需额外训练或形态学标注即可生成厚血膜疟疾检测的可解释形态学证据，为显微镜检查人员提供了审计深度学习模型预测的工具，填补了自动检测与临床可解释性之间的空白。

Abstract: Malaria diagnosis in endemic regions depends on species-level identification of Plasmodium parasites in thick blood smears, but deep learning detectors classify detections without providing morphological evidence for their predictions, limiting the ability of microscopists to audit those predictions at the case level. We present SGMCE (Segment-Grounded Morphological Concept Explanation), a post-hoc explanation framework that requires no additional training, no morphological annotations, and no labelled explanation data, yet produces per-detection natural-language explanations anchored in thick-smear morphology. For each detection, SGMCE extracts mask-guided crop thumbnails, computes fourteen handcrafted computer-vision morphological features (shape, colour, chromatin, haemozoin pigment) using adaptive within-mask thresholds, and queries GPT-4o with both visual evidence and computed measurements, conditioned on a thick-smear-specific knowledge base compiled from the World Health Organization bench aids. The primary output is a structured explanation identifying which morphological features support the detected species and why the competing species are excluded. Explanations are validated by four automatic metrics: Knowledge-Base Consistency (KBC), CV-Claim Faithfulness (CCF), Discriminativeness Score (DS), and LLM-as-Judge (LLMj). A sentence-level semantic scoring rule with species-aware negation filtering resolves the vocabulary mismatch between clinical prose and knowledge-base terms. Across 737 detections from 139 thick-smear images spanning four Plasmodium species and white blood cells, parasite-class mean KBC is 0.91, mean DS is 0.99, and mean CCF is 0.97, while a per-rule CCF breakdown confirms that the CV-grounded claims made by the vision-language model are consistent with the measurements they cite.

</details>


### [41] [RegionFM: Interpretable Region-Based Brain MRI Classification Using Foundation Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16325)
*Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RegionFM，一种将解剖学分割与脑MRI基础模型嵌入相结合的框架，通过区域可加性逻辑模型为每个解剖区域分配显式标量贡献，在保持competitive预测性能的同时提供符合解剖学结构的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑MRI基础模型的预测难以用解剖学术语解释，传统的体素或补丁级重要性图无法显式量化单个脑区域的贡献，与临床基于解剖结构的评估方式不匹配。

Method: 1) 将MRI扫描分割为解剖区域，为每个区域构建独立MRI体块；2) 用冻结的基础模型编码每个区域得到嵌入；3) 通过区域可加性逻辑模型组合嵌入，使每个解剖区域对最终预测贡献一个显式标量。支持个体级和队列级分析。

Result: 在认知障碍分类任务中，RegionFM在多个预训练脑MRI基础模型上保持了与可解释性较差的微调方法相当的性能；随机嵌入消融实验性能接近随机水平，表明预测依赖基础模型嵌入捕获的有意义结构。

Conclusion: RegionFM在保持competitive预测性能的同时，使模型解释与基于解剖学的临床推理更好对齐。

Abstract: Foundation models provide powerful representations for brain MRI analysis, but their predictions remain difficult to interpret in anatomically meaningful terms. Clinical assessment of brain MRI is commonly organized around anatomically defined structures and regional abnormalities, whereas conventional explanation methods typically produce voxel- or patch-level importance maps that do not explicitly quantify the contributions of individual brain regions. To address this mismatch, we propose RegionFM, an interpretable framework that integrates anatomical segmentation with brain MRI foundation-model embeddings. RegionFM first divides each MRI scan into anatomical regions and constructs a separate MRI volume for each region. A frozen foundation model then encodes each region into an embedding, and a region-additive logistic model combines these embeddings such that every anatomical region contributes an explicit scalar term to the final prediction. This formulation supports both subject-level and cohort-level analyses of regional contributions. We evaluate RegionFM on cognitive-impairment classification using embeddings from multiple pretrained brain MRI foundation models. The results show that RegionFM maintains performance comparable to less interpretable fine-tuning approaches while providing anatomically grounded explanations. Randomized embedding ablations yield near-chance performance, indicating that the predictions rely on meaningful structure captured by the foundation-model embeddings rather than simple feature statistics. Overall, RegionFM better aligns model explanations with anatomy-based clinical reasoning while maintaining competitive predictive performance.

</details>


### [42] [CRISP: Pre-LLM Yet Text-Driven Visual Token Pruning for Efficient LVLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16326)
*Xu Li,Yi Zheng,Mengyang Zhao,Yuxuan Liang,Zhe Liu,Rui Zhu,Xiaolei Chen,Wei Zhou,Baoquan Zhao,Juncen Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CRISP是一种在LLM之前进行的、文本驱动的视觉令牌剪枝框架，通过两阶段流程（文本对齐令牌识别+语义多样性增强）在保持高达99.5%准确率的同时将推理成本和延迟降低2倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉令牌剪枝方法要么在LLM之前使用文本无关的启发式方法，要么在LLM内部剪枝但存在效率低和跨模态注意力噪声的问题，缺乏一种既高效又能保留指令相关信息与场景上下文的预LLM文本驱动剪枝方法。

Method: 提出CRISP两阶段框架：Stage 1识别与文本对齐的视觉令牌，Stage 2通过语义多样性增强上下文完整性，在进入LLM之前完成文本驱动的视觉令牌剪枝。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5和LLaVA-NeXT上，CRISP在激进剪枝率下保持高达99.5%的准确率，同时将推理成本和延迟降低超过2倍。

Conclusion: CRISP是一种实用的高效LVLM推理解决方案，特别适用于资源受限场景。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) typically require processing hundreds to thousands of visual tokens, leading to substantial inference overhead. Existing visual token pruning methods either operate before the LLM using text-agnostic heuristics or prune inside the LLM at the cost of efficiency and noisy cross-modal attention. To address these limitations, we propose CRISP, a pre-LLM yet text-driven visual token pruning framework that preserves both instruction-relevant evidence and essential scene context. CRISP works in a two-stage pipeline: Stage 1 first identifies text-aligned visual tokens, and Stage 2 enhances contextual completeness through semantic diversity. Extensive experiments on LLaVA-1.5 and LLaVA-NeXT demonstrate that CRISP achieves superior performance retention under aggressive pruning ratios, maintaining up to 99.5% accuracy while reducing inference cost and latency by more than 2 times. CRISP serves as a practical solution for efficient LVLM inference, especially in resource-constrained scenarios.

</details>


### [43] [Localization-Infused Vision-Language Semantic Fusion for Text-Guided Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16327)
*Songyue Han,Mingye Zou,Shuchang Ye,Lei Bi,Mingyuan Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LoG框架，通过显式捕获目标位置信息和三层次定位引导的语义融合策略，实现文本引导的医学图像分割，在三个基准数据集上均达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导分割方法未能显式捕获文本报告中的目标导向信息（特别是目标位置），且仅采用简单的特征级融合，缺乏多级别信息融合策略，限制了关键文本语义的提取与整合。

Method: 提出LoG（Localization-infused Vision-Language Fusion）框架，联合执行多尺度目标定位任务以显式捕获目标导向的视觉-语言语义，并实现三层次定位引导的语义融合：(i) 定位引导特征融合，(ii) 定位门控注意力融合，(iii) 定位约束损失融合。

Result: 在QaTa-COV19、MosMedData+和Kvasir-SEG三个数据集上分别达到91.59%、80.71%和94.59%的Dice分数，一致优于现有最先进的医学图像分割方法。

Conclusion: 显式捕获文本中的目标位置信息并进行多层次定位引导的语义融合，能有效提升文本引导医学图像分割的性能。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation is essential for modern computer-aided medicine. Recently, text-guided segmentation has shown promise by incorporating clinician-formulated textual reports as semantic guidance for image segmentation. These reports describe target appearance, location, and neighboring anatomy, providing explicit guidance for localization and delineation. Existing text-guided segmentation methods typically extract textual semantics implicitly through a pretrained text encoder and then integrate vision-language semantics via straightforward image-text feature fusion. However, these methods do not explicitly capture target-oriented information embedded in textual reports, particularly target location, and do not explore multi-level information fusion strategies beyond basic feature-level fusion, limiting the extraction and integration of critical textual semantics. In this study, we propose LoG, a localization-infused vision-language fusion framework for text-guided medical image segmentation. By jointly performing multi-scale target localization tasks, LoG explicitly captures target-oriented vision-language semantics and enables three-level localization-infused semantic fusion: (i) localization-guided feature fusion that directly infuses location-relevant semantics into visual features, (ii) localization-gated attention fusion that redirects multi-scale localization predictions to reinforce critical regions, and (iii) localization-constrained loss fusion that supervises segmentation based on spatial consistency with target localization. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets, involving three medical imaging modalities with paired textual reports, demonstrate that LoG achieves Dice scores of 91.59%, 80.71%, and 94.59% on QaTa-COV19, MosMedData+, and Kvasir-SEG, respectively, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art medical image segmentation methods.

</details>


### [44] [Local Brushstroke Quality Assessment via Vision-Language Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16330)
*Mio Mitamura,Hirokatsu Kataoka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探索多模态大语言模型（GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4、Gemini 2.5 Flash）评估书法局部笔触质量并生成教育性反馈的能力。模型对书法作品前后对比图像进行五点量表评分，与三位专家评分对比。GPT-4o绝对准确率最优（MAE=0.885），但所有模型与专家的总体秩相关均无统计显著性。RAG变体提高了秩相关性却降低了绝对准确率，构成基于文本规则注入的负面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证多模态大语言模型能否像人类专家一样评估书法笔触质量，并生成可用于书法教学的反馈，探索AI在艺术教育评估领域的可行性。

Method: 构建评估框架：三个多模态LLM（GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4、Gemini 2.5 Flash）对书法作品前后对比图像进行五点李克特量表评分；与三位书法专家评分对比；引入RAG变体作为前置条件；使用MAE评估绝对准确率，Kendall's tau评估秩相关性，并进行生成理据的词汇分析。

Result: GPT-4o取得最佳MAE（0.885），所有模型达到有用水平的绝对评分准确率；但所有模型与人类专家的总体秩相关均未达统计显著性；各模型在生成理据中存在特征性评估偏差；RAG提升秩相关但降低绝对准确率。

Conclusion: 当前多模态LLM在书法笔触评估的绝对准确性上表现尚可，但无法复现人类专家在作品间相对排序上的判断；基于文本规则注入的RAG方法存在权衡效应，并非改进方向的有效策略。

Abstract: This paper investigates whether multimodal LLMs can evaluate local brushstroke quality in calligraphy and generate educationally useful natural language feedback. We construct an evaluation framework in which three multimodal LLMs (GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4, and Gemini 2.5 Flash) assess before-after image pairs of calligraphic works using a five-point ordinal scale, and compare their outputs against scores assigned by three expert calligraphers. We additionally examine a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) variant of Claude as a preliminary condition. Results show that all models achieve useful levels of absolute score accuracy (MAE), with GPT-4o performing best (MAE = 0.885). However, none of the models produce statistically significant overall rank correlations with human experts (Kendall's tau). Vocabulary analysis of generated rationales reveals characteristic evaluative biases in each model, and RAG is shown to improve rank correlation while worsening absolute accuracy, constituting an important negative result for text-based rule injection.

</details>


### [45] [DMFNet: Dual-Backbone Multiscale Fusion Network for Urban Scene Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16338)
*Anamitra Ghosh,Abhiroop Chatterjee,Susmita Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DMFNet，一种双骨干多尺度特征融合框架，结合残差特征传播和空间注意力用于遥感场景分类。在AID数据集上取得97.46%±0.14%的平均精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以有效捕获多尺度特征交互，且难以从复杂航拍场景中学习鲁棒特征表示，尤其是面对高类内变异性和高类间相似性时。

Method: 采用两个预训练骨干网络提取层次化特征；提出带残差特征传播的多尺度特征融合机制增强跨分辨率特征交互；引入空间注意力模块强调多目标场景中的信息区域；采用两阶段训练策略（骨干冻结+选择性微调）。

Result: 在AID基准数据集上平均准确率达97.46%±0.14%，消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: DMFNet通过双骨干多尺度融合与注意力机制有效提升了遥感场景分类性能，两阶段训练策略确保了稳定优化与泛化能力。

Abstract: This article presents DMFNet, a dual-backbone multiscale feature fusion framework with residual feature propagation and spatial attention for remote sensing scene classification. Existing approaches often face challenges in effectively capturing multiscale feature interactions and learning robust feature representations from complex aerial scenes with high intra-class variability and inter-class similarity. To address these limitations, the proposed framework employs two pretrained backbone networks to extract diverse hierarchical feature representations. A multiscale feature fusion mechanism with residual feature propagation is introduced to enhance feature interaction across multiple resolution levels. In addition, a spatial attention module is introduced to emphasize informative spatial regions in multi-object scenes. Further, a two-stage training strategy consisting of backbone freezing followed by selective fine-tuning is adopted to ensure stable optimization and improved generalization. Experiments conducted on the benchmark AID dataset demonstrate that the DMFNet achieves an average accuracy of 97.46\% $\pm$ 0.14\%. Ablative analysis further show the importance of various components in unison.

</details>


### [46] [Privacy-Aware Synthetic Video Benchmarking and Relational Evaluation for Worker-Under-Suspended-Load Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16351)
*Anshu Singh,Alejandro Seif*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对建筑工地工人处于悬挂荷载下的危险性关系识别任务，本文提出了一个合成的视频基准数据集SynthSite（55个剪辑），并研究了在五种全身隐私条件下，结构保持的模糊化方法相比外观平滑方法能更好地保留下游实用性，同时指出隐私评估不仅要看外观抑制效果，还需评估对几何线索的保留程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开可共享的建筑施工视频基准稀缺，尤其是涉及安全关键的危险场景（罕见、危险且难以拍摄），且现有方法仅依赖目标检测，无法捕捉工人-荷载间的几何关系和时空持续性这一关系型危险。

Method: 1) 提出SynthSite合成视频基准，包含55个剪辑，覆盖多种荷载配置、视角、遮挡和监控条件；2) 设计隐私感知的混合生成工作流，支持公开共享和隐私受限场景；3) 在五种全身隐私条件下评估工人与荷载的保留度、定位稳定性和剪辑级危险识别性能。

Result: 结构保持的模糊化方法相比外观平滑基线保留了显著更多的下游实用性；仅保留原始视觉参考并不能保证与人类危险标签的最高一致性。

Conclusion: 建筑施工安全分析中的隐私评估应同时考虑外观抑制和危险推理所需几何线索的保留，而不仅仅是外观层面的保护。

Abstract: Publicly shareable construction-video benchmarks remain scarce, especially for safety-critical hazards that are rare, dangerous to stage, and difficult to release. We study worker under suspended load, a relational hazard that depends on worker-load geometry and temporal persistence rather than object detection alone. We introduce SynthSite, a focused synthetic video benchmark of 55 clips spanning varied load configurations, viewpoints, clutter, occlusions, and surveillance conditions, together with a privacy-aware hybrid generation workflow that supports both publicly shareable benchmark creation and privacy-constrained synthetic video generation.
  We then ask whether worker appearance can be suppressed without undermining downstream hazard recognition. Under five whole-body privacy conditions, we evaluate worker and load retention, localization stability, and clip-level hazard recognition. We find that structure-preserving obfuscations retain substantially more downstream utility than appearance-smoothing baselines, and that preserving a raw visual reference alone does not guarantee the strongest agreement with human hazard labels. These findings suggest that privacy evaluation for construction safety analytics should assess not only appearance suppression, but also preservation of the geometric cues required for hazard reasoning. Our dataset and code are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/govtech/SynthSite .

</details>


### [47] [Clarify Before Executing: A Self-Evolving Agent for Resolving Intent Asymmetry in 3D Tool Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16352)
*Xiaoye Zhu,Weixin Li,Junan Huo,Bozhong Wang,Jia Zeng,Yi Yang,Cen Chen,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLARE是一种基于澄清感知的进化式3D智能体，通过将生成流程解耦为四个认知角色并采用多轮对话自进化策略，解决了用户意图模糊与3D工具精确执行之间的不对称问题，在620个场景的基准测试中取得了60.40%和43.34%的单步与多步任务成功率，性能超越现有方法两倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D工具链要求精确可执行的参数，但普通用户通常提供模糊、不明确的指令。当前的3D智能体将这种意图不对称视为噪声，默认在单轮交互假设下盲目执行，导致任务失败。

Method: 将生成流水线解耦为四种专门认知角色，以拦截并解析不明确的指令；通过模拟多轮交互自进化澄清策略；引入多轮奖励优化智能体在交互效率与任务完成之间的平衡；构建包含620个交互场景的3D-Clarify基准测试集。

Result: CLARE在单步任务中达到60.40%成功率，在多步任务中达到43.34%成功率，性能是现有基线的两倍以上，在文本到3D生成、单视图重建、多视图重建、点云编辑和后处理五个领域均取得最优结果。

Conclusion: 主动澄清是实现稳健3D执行的关键，意图不对称不应被视为执行错误，而应视为战略性对话的机会。

Abstract: A fundamental intent asymmetry plagues modern 3D asset creation: while state-of-the-art 3D toolchains demand precise, executable parameters, ordinary users typically provide vague, underspecified instructions. Current 3D agents treat this ambiguity as noise, defaulting to blind execution under a single-turn assumption. To address this limitation, we introduce CLARE, a clarification-aware and evolutionary 3D agent that treats intent asymmetry not as an execution error, but as an opportunity for strategic dialogue. By decoupling the generation pipeline into four specialized cognitive roles, CLARE intercepts and resolves underspecified instructions before invoking computationally expensive 3D tools to seamlessly execute tasks across five diverse domains: text-to-3D generation, single-view reconstruction, multi-view reconstruction, point cloud editing, and post-processing. Crucially, rather than relying on rigid manual rules, CLARE self-evolves its clarification policy via simulated multi-turn interactions. By optimizing a Multi-turn Reward, the agent internalizes the delicate balance between interaction efficiency and task completion. To rigorously test this, we construct 3D-Clarify, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 620 interaction scenarios with systematically injected ambiguity, missing information, and mistaken details. CLARE achieves state-of-the-art performance, with 60.40% and 43.34% success rates on single-step and multi-step tasks, respectively, more than doubling existing baselines. Both quantitative and qualitative results demonstrate that proactive clarification is the missing key to robust 3D execution. Code is available at https://github.com/xyzhu1225/CLARE.

</details>


### [48] [PhysAgent: Reflective Agentic Physics Control for Physically Plausible Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16355)
*Qirui Li,Jinkun Hao,Yibo Li,Ran Yi,Paul L. Rosin,Yu-Kun Lai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysAgent是一个反射式智能体框架，通过将物理程序生成、物理模拟、分阶段验证和针对性程序修复形成闭环，相比单次预测方法，能更准确地生成物理上合理的视频，实现复杂的轨迹、多阶段交互和精确的事件结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于物理的视频生成方法依赖视觉语言模型进行单次物理参数预测，难以准确将用户意图转化为可执行的物理模拟，尤其在细粒度物体动态、复杂运动轨迹和时间结构化交互方面表现不佳。

Method: 提出PhysAgent反射式智能体框架，在物理程序生成、物理模拟、阶段验证和程序修复之间形成闭环；将每个物理程序视为可执行假设，使智能体逐步实现复杂轨迹和多阶段交互；设计物理控制API以支持更稳定复杂的运动行为。

Result: PhysAgent生成更物理合理的视频，实现更好的提示对齐，并在多种物理场景中展现出更有效的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过闭环反射机制，PhysAgent显著提升了物理视频生成中对耦合参数的控制精度、复杂运动轨迹的实现能力以及跨场景泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in physics-grounded video generation leverage physics simulation as a physical prior to guide video synthesis toward physically plausible outcomes. The simulation process is controlled by physical specifications, which are typically generated by a vision-language model in a single pass. Such one-shot prediction often fails to accurately translate user intent into executable simulations, particularly for fine-grained object dynamics, complex motion trajectories, and temporally structured interactions. In this paper, we propose PhysAgent, a reflective agentic framework that closes the loop among physical program generation, physics simulation, stage-specific verification, and targeted program repair. Beyond improving the control of coupled physical parameters, our framework enables the agent to progressively realize complex trajectories, multi-stage interactions, and precise event outcomes by treating each physical program as an executable hypothesis. In addition, we design a set of physics-control APIs to support more stable and complex motion behaviors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PhysAgent produces more physically plausible videos, achieves better prompt alignment, and generalizes more effectively across diverse physical scenarios.

</details>


### [49] [Spatial Transport of Integration Error in Generative ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16361)
*Songheng Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过符号化的源-传输核算方法，系统分析了扩散/流模型在少步采样中积分误差的来源、传播路径及最终端点误差的构成，发现误差主要来自其他区域的传输而非本地注入，并揭示了模型速度场沿轨迹的变化结构可预测区域误差，同时提出了基于该结构的训练惩罚来降低少步误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散/流模型在少步求解时，积分误差在图像上分布不均匀，但误差的来源、传播机制及如何到达端点尚不明确。本文旨在通过符号化的源-传输核算，定位误差注入位置并追踪其传播路径，以理解少步集成误差的形成机制。

Method: 采用符号化的源-传输核算（signed source-and-transport accounting）方法，对少步积分误差进行一阶测试。通过对五个256^2分辨率模型进行扰动实验，分析误差传播特性；通过线性化动力学传播单步截断残差，重建端点误差的方向和区域结构；通过结构破坏消融实验（随机化贡献符号、重新分配接收区域）验证核算的完整性；分析模型速度/预测场沿轨迹的变化结构与区域误差的相关性。

Result: 1. 采样初期局部扰动的端点响应中，不到10%保留在源位置；2. 单步截断残差经线性化动力学传播后，与端点误差的余弦相似度达0.81-0.87；3. 区域误差主要来自其他区域的传输贡献；4. 随机化贡献符号使误差减半，重新分配接收区域则完全破坏；5. 模型速度场沿轨迹的变化结构与最终区域误差的相关性rho为0.57-0.70；6. 对该变化结构施加训练惩罚可降低少步误差。

Conclusion: 扩散/流模型少步采样中，端点误差主要源于误差在区域间的广泛传播而非本地注入，模型内部的速度场变化结构（训练中涌现）可部分预测区域误差，且该结构可通过训练优化以降低少步采样误差。

Abstract: A trained flow or diffusion model is usually run with only a handful of solver steps, and the integration error this leaves behind is unevenly distributed across the image. We ask where that error is injected and how it reaches the endpoint, and answer with a signed source-and-transport accounting of few-step integration error, tested to first order. A perturbation experiment on five models at 256^2 resolution shows the learned dynamics spread local disturbances widely: near the start of sampling, under 10% of the summed endpoint response remains at the source. Signed one-step truncation residuals, propagated through the model's own linearized dynamics, reconstruct much of the endpoint error's direction and regional structure (cosine 0.81-0.87), and a region's error owes more to what arrives from elsewhere than to its own injection. Structure-destroying nulls, with protocols frozen before evaluation, locate what carries the account: randomizing contribution signs halves it, and reassigning which region receives each contribution, with content, norms, and signs intact, destroys it entirely. Where the injections land is readable from the model itself. The variation of its velocity or prediction field along the trajectory, a structure that emerges during training, predicts the final per-region gap (within-image rho of 0.57-0.70 on fine trajectories, weaker from the cheap solve alone). The prediction is partial because endpoint error depends not only on injected magnitude but on its sign, timing, and transport through the learned dynamics. A training penalty on the injected variation lowers few-step error, so the structure is one a model can be trained to change.

</details>


### [50] [OmniStyle-INR: Universal and Multimodal Style Transfer for INRs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16362)
*Rafał Kajca,Michał Miziołek,Kornel Howil,Rafał Tobiasz,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniStyle-INR框架，利用隐式神经表示(INR)作为统一域，实现对2D图像、视频、3D场景和4D动态的全模态高质量风格迁移，支持文本和视觉参考图像双引导。相比基于高斯泼溅的方法，INR在密集连续域中具有更优的结构适配性、数据压缩、超分辨率和深度生成模型集成等优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯泼溅(Gaussian Splatting)的风格迁移方法在表示2D图像和视频时结构上存在次优性，所需高斯数量接近像素总数，实用性存疑；而隐式神经表示(INR)在所有视觉模态中更为自然和流行，具备数据压缩、超分辨率和与深度生成模型无缝集成等优势。因此，作者希望利用INR作为真正的统一域来实现跨模态风格迁移。

Method: 提出OmniStyle-INR框架，采用基于网络的连续表示（隐式神经表示）作为统一域，同时支持文本提示和视觉示例（参考图像）作为引导条件，对所有视觉模态（2D图像、视频、3D场景、4D动态）进行风格迁移。

Result: 成功实现了跨所有视觉模态的高质量风格迁移，在文本和视觉双引导条件下均表现出色。

Conclusion: 隐式神经表示作为统一域在跨模态风格迁移任务中优于高斯泼溅方法，具有更好的结构适配性和实用价值。

Abstract: Style transfer remains a fundamental and highly important task across various data modalities, enabling creative manipulation conditioned by both reference images and textual descriptions. Recently, methods utilizing Gaussian Splatting have emerged as a unified representation for 2D images, video, 3D scenes, and 4D dynamics. However, representing videos and 2D images with Gaussian Splatting is structurally sub-optimal for dense continuous domains. The number of required Gaussians often approaches the total number of pixels, raising questions about the actual utility of such a representation for these specific modalities. In contrast, Implicit Neural Representations have established themselves as a much more popular and natural choice across all these data domains. Implicit Neural Representations naturally provide significant advantages, including data compression, inherent capabilities for super resolution, and seamless integration with deep generative models. To this end, we introduce OmniStyle-INR, a novel framework that leverages network-based continuous representations as a truly universal domain. Our approach successfully performs high-quality style transfer across all visual modalities, guided seamlessly by both text prompts and visual exemplars.

</details>


### [51] [Disentangling Model and Human Data Uncertainty in Apparent Facial Age Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16378)
*Andrei Foitos,Ivo Pascal de Jong,Matias Valdenegro-Toro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了人脸图像表观年龄估计中的不确定性量化问题，通过训练贝叶斯神经网络（MC-DropConnect、Flipout、Deep Ensembles），利用APPA-REAL数据集中的主观年龄标注，将不确定性分解为认知不确定性（因知识缺乏）和偶然不确定性（因内在噪声）。实验验证了偶然不确定性随数据集规模保持稳定，而认知不确定性在数据减少时增加的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸表观年龄估计具有主观性和数据内在变异性，现有方法缺乏对不确定性来源的归因与量化分析，尤其是未能区分认知不确定性和偶然不确定性在年龄估计任务中的表现规律。

Method: 使用APPA-REAL数据集，训练三种贝叶斯神经网络近似方法（MC-DropConnect、Flipout、Deep Ensembles），在不同规模训练集上监督学习人类标注的偶然不确定性，模型同时输出预测年龄及两类不确定性估计。

Result: 偶然不确定性在不同数据集规模下保持稳定，认知不确定性随训练数据减少而增加，验证了不确定性分解假设的有效性。

Conclusion: 人脸年龄估计中可量化不同来源的不确定性，为模型可靠性评估和不确定性感知学习提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Estimating the apparent age of individuals from facial images is challenging due to the subjective nature of perception and the inherent variability of the data. We investigate the role of uncertainty estimation, attributing uncertainty jointly to a lack of knowledge (epistemic) or inherent noise/chance (aleatoric). Leveraging the APPA-REAL dataset, we train Bayesian Neural Networks on datasets of varying sizes using three BNN approximations: MC-DropConnect, Flipout, and Deep Ensembles using supervision on human aleatoric uncertainty available in the APPA-REAL dataset. Each model outputs both the predicted apparent age and the amount of aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty. Our results confirm the hypothesis that the inherent aleatoric uncertainty remains stable across dataset sizes, while epistemic uncertainty increases as training data decreases. These findings demonstrate that different sources of uncertainty can be quantified in face age estimation.

</details>


### [52] [Apple-$π$: Benchmarking Thinking with Video Towards Law-Grounded Physical Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16401)
*Runmao Yao,Kairui Hu,Yukang Cao,Ruisi Wang,Shulin Tian,Ziang Cao,Weichen Fan,Ziqi Huang,Yuhao Dong,Hao Li,Zhaoxi Chen,Zhongang Cai,Lei Yang,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Apple-PI，首个基于物理定律对视频生成模型进行诊断性评估的基准。包含400个经典力学视频数据集、三阶段科学推理评估协议（感知-表述-演绎）以及混合评估体系。对11个模型的评测显示，最佳模型得分仅0.473，暴露了当前视频模型在物理定律推理上的显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型被宣称具备物理规律内化能力，但现有基准仅停留在输出层面对物理合理性进行评估，无法验证模型是否通过忠实、符合物理定律的推理过程生成结果。作者旨在建立可诊断模型在物理推理各环节具体失败位置的评估框架。

Method: 1) Orchard数据集：400个视频覆盖10个经典力学任务，分离单定律任务（消除混杂因素）和多定律任务（测试泛化能力）。2) 三阶段基准协议：感知（Perception）、表述（Formulation）、演绎（Deduction），采用信息图标注首帧的链式帧提示，将生成视频视为模型的可视化推理轨迹。3) 混合评估套件：结合多模态大语言模型主观评分和基于物理定律的客观度量，实现分阶段诊断。

Result: 对11个模型的基准测试显示，最佳视频模型得分仅0.473，远非可靠的基于物理定律的世界模拟器。阶段分析揭示了感知→表述→演绎的瓶颈，以及多定律状态转移能力弱、持续的模拟到真实（Sim-to-Real）差距等问题。

Conclusion: Apple-PI可作为指导未来视频模型向具备基于物理定律的物理智能发展的诊断基础，其分阶段、分支柱、分来源的分析方法能够精确定位模型在物理推理链条中的具体失败环节。

Abstract: Modern video generation models are increasingly hailed as emerging world models with an internalized grasp of physical law. Yet existing benchmarks largely evaluate physical plausibility only at the output level, without verifying whether the model arrives there through a faithful, law-grounded reasoning process. We introduce Apple-PI, the first benchmark that anchors video-model evaluation explicitly in physical laws. Apple-PI comprises three components. 1) Orchard: a dataset of 400 videos covering ten canonical tasks in classical mechanics. It separates single-law tasks for confounder-free diagnosis from multi-law tasks for probing generalization. 2) Benchmark Protocol: a three-stage protocol based on scientific reasoning, including Perception, Formulation, and Deduction. It uses chain-of-frames prompting on infographic-annotated first frames, treating the generated video as the model's visible reasoning trace. 3) Evaluation Suite: a hybrid evaluation suite that combines MLLM-based subjective scoring with physics-law-grounded objective measures. This enables stage-resolved diagnosis of not only whether a model fails, but where it fails. Benchmarking 11 models shows that current video models remain far from reliable law-grounded world simulators, with the best video model scoring only 0.473. Our stage-, pillar-, and source-resolved analyses further expose a Perception-to-Formulation-to-Deduction bottleneck, weak multi-law state transfer, and a persistent Sim-to-Real gap. These findings position Apple-PI as a diagnostic foundation for guiding future video models toward world models with law-grounded physical intelligence.

</details>


### [53] [Think, Plan, Paint: Layout-Aware Reasoning for Controllable Image Generation in Unified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16409)
*Junhao Liu,Jian-Wei Zhang,Tao Huang,Miles Yang,Zhao Zhong,Liefeng Bo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ATLAS是一个统一的多模态大语言模型框架，采用"思考-规划-绘制"范式，通过布局作为共享表示来连接空间推理、目标规划与图像生成三个环节，并利用强化学习提升布局到图像的保真度，在7B和80B规模上均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态大语言模型在可控图像生成中难以遵循复杂的空间指令和逻辑约束，缺乏系统性的空间推理与规划能力。

Method: 提出ATLAS框架，采用"Think, Plan, and Paint"范式，以布局（layout）作为三个阶段的共享表征，并在plan-to-image阶段引入基于强化学习的布局对齐来提升生成保真度。

Result: 在7B和80B规模上实现图像生成基准的SOTA性能，相比基于布局的统一MLLMs平均提升65.31%，在空间相关任务上平均提升23.06%。

Conclusion: ATLAS通过引入类人的"思考-规划-绘制"范式与布局共享表征，显著提升了MLLMs在复杂空间指令下的可控图像生成能力，同时通过统一布局接口支持指令引导编辑与多模态定位等下游任务。

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) offer a promising paradigm for unifying visual understanding and generation, yet they still struggle to follow complex spatial instructions and logical constraints in controllable image generation. To address this gap, we present ATLAS, a unified framework that equips MLLMs with a human-like "Think, Plan, and Paint" paradigm. We adopt layout as the shared representation that connects the three stages, enabling the model to reason about spatial requirements, plan explicit object arrangements, and render the final image. We further improve plan-to-image fidelity with reinforcement-learning-based layout alignment. We instantiate ATLAS at 7B and 80B scales, achieving state-of-the-art performance among MLLMs on image generation benchmarks and an average 65.31% improvement over existing layout-based unified MLLMs. On spatially related tasks, ATLAS obtains an average 23.06% gain over the base models. Through the same layout interface, ATLAS also supports instruction-guided editing and multimodal grounding. We further introduce ATLAS-Reasoning, a benchmark for evaluating generation under complex spatial instructions.

</details>


### [54] [One Modality to Forget Them All: Enhancing Cross-Modal Unlearning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16442)
*Sudharshan Balaji,Yili Ren,Guangjing Wang,Yimin Chen,Ning Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了视觉-语言模型(VLM)中跨模态遗忘迁移问题，发现遗忘在文本和视觉模态间存在不对称且不完全的迁移，且易受排版攻击恢复。为此提出CrossInf策略，通过影响引导聚焦于对跨模态泛化影响最大的Transformer块进行遗忘，将迁移差距减少一半以上，并将排版攻击成功率降至接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VLM同时处理文本和视觉输入，但遗忘仅在一个模态进行时是否能迁移到另一模态尚不清楚。本文旨在系统探究这一安全问题的本质。

Method: 在三种VLM架构（LLaVA-1.5、InstructBLIP、IDEFICS）上进行双向跨模态遗忘迁移实验；提出CrossInf策略，基于影响引导（Influence-guided）选择对跨模态泛化影响最大的Transformer块进行集中遗忘；使用CKA分析浅层遗忘现象。

Result: 跨模态遗忘是存在的，但不对称且不完全；文本遗忘可强迁移到视觉，但在排版攻击下可恢复；CrossInf将跨模态迁移差距减少一半以上，同时保持模型效用；排版攻击成功率降至接近零；人类评估一致性κ=0.77。

Conclusion: VLM中的跨模态遗忘存在显著迁移差距和浅层鲁棒性问题，CrossInf策略能有效缓解该问题，为多模态模型安全遗忘提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Machine unlearning is widely used to remove hazardous knowledge from large language models. Modern Vision-Language Models (VLMs), however, process both text and visual inputs, raising a fundamental security question: does unlearning in one modality transfer to the other? We present the first systematic, bidirectional study of cross-modal unlearning transfer across three VLM architectures: LLaVA-1.5 (MLP projection), InstructBLIP (Q-Former), and IDEFICS (gated cross-attention). We find that unlearning transfers across modalities, but the transfer is asymmetric and incomplete. In some cases, text unlearning strongly transfers to vision. However, this robustness is not preserved under typographic attacks that manipulate the visual presentation of text. Under such attacks, previously unlearned knowledge can be readily recovered, indicating shallow unlearning.
  To address the transfer gap and shallow robustness, we propose \textsc{CrossInf}, an influence-guided mitigation strategy. Motivated by the observation that different model components contribute unequally to cross-modal transfer, \textsc{CrossInf} focuses unlearning on transformer blocks that most influence cross-modal generalization. It reduces the transfer gap by more than half in architectures with strong fusion, while preserving model utility. It also improves robustness under typographic attacks, reducing the attack success rate to near zero. We further conduct human evaluation with three annotators ($κ{=}0.77$) to validate our findings. Finally, we analyze shallow unlearning using Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA), providing insights into the observed transfer behavior and robustness limitations.

</details>


### [55] [DS@GT ARC at AnimalCLEF 2026: Species-Aware Graph Construction for Multi-Species Animal Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16453)
*Evan Sinclair Smith,Anthony Miyaguchi,Snigdha Palamari,Danté Evangelista*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多物种动物个体重识别系统，通过物种感知图构建、LightGlue局部验证、LightGBM配对评分和Leiden社区检测等模块，在AnimalCLEF 2026竞赛中取得第五名（公开ARI 0.733，私有ARI 0.674），证明了全局相似性、局部特征、邻域上下文和图级约束的协同整合对于鲁棒野生动物重识别的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外图像中个体身份线索与姿态、光照、背景、分辨率及物种形态等干扰因素难以分离，且基于聚类的重识别方法存在高分错误对作为桥接边导致不同个体合并的问题。

Method: 将重识别建模为物种感知图构建问题，整合定制预处理、全局候选检索、基于LightGlue的多关键点族局部验证、LightGBM配对评分、保守边准入策略及Leiden社区检测。

Result: 公开ARI 0.733，私有ARI 0.674，在230支参赛队伍中排名第五。消融实验表明局部特征支持、前景感知预处理和物种特异性骨干网络能增强配对证据，而图操作点决定碎片化与过度合并之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的野生动物重识别不仅需要强视觉表征，还需要全局相似性、局部身份标记、邻域上下文和图级约束的校准整合。

Abstract: Automated individual animal re-identification is essential for large-scale biodiversity monitoring; however, field imagery complicates separating identity cues from nuisance variation in pose, illumination, background, resolution, and species-specific morphology. The DS@GT ARC submission to AnimalCLEF 2026 introduces a multi-species image-clustering system for re-identifying Eurasian lynx, fire salamanders, loggerhead sea turtles, and Texas horned lizards. Instead of relying on a single descriptor or nearest-neighbor retrieval, this approach formulates re-identification as species-aware graph construction over candidate image pairs. The pipeline integrates tailored preprocessing, global candidate retrieval, LightGlue-based local verification with multiple keypoint families, LightGBM pair scoring, conservative edge admission, and Leiden community detection. This design directly addresses a primary failure mode of clustering-based re-identification: high-scoring false pairs that act as bridge edges and merge distinct individuals through transitive closure. Across species, ablation studies demonstrate that local feature support, foreground-aware preprocessing, and species-specific backbone selection enhance pair evidence, while graph operating points determine the trade-off between fragmentation and over-merging. The selected submission achieved a public ARI of 0.733 and a private ARI of 0.674, ranking fifth among 230 teams. These results indicate that robust wildlife re-identification requires not only strong visual representations but also calibrated integration of global similarity, local identity markings, neighborhood context, and graph-level constraints. The code can be found at https://github.com/dsgt-arc/animalclef-2026.

</details>


### [56] [Spectral-Morphological Attention U-Net: An Efficient Network for Active Wildfire Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16472)
*Yugong Zeng,Jonathan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SMA-UNet的新型深度学习模型，通过集成光谱注意力模块、残差注意力UNet主干、通道-空间调制器和可微分形态学门控，在卫星图像 wildfire 检测任务上取得了最优性能（TS-SatFire数据集IoU 75.16%，Sen2Fire数据集IoU 22.50%），其中可微分形态学门控是首次创新性地应用于活跃火点检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球野火频率持续增长，早期精准检测与定位对最大限度减少潜在危害至关重要。基于卫星图像的机器学习方法能够自动监测极其偏远和广阔的区域，在野火检测领域展现出巨大应用潜力。

Method: 提出了SMA-UNet模型，包含四个核心组件：光谱注意力模块、残差注意力UNet主干、通道-空间调制器以及一对可微分形态学门控。除主干外，其余模块均为首次应用于活跃火点检测，其中可微分形态学门控为创新性开发。

Result: 在两个数据集上取得最优结果：TS-SatFire数据集上IoU为75.16%，Sen2Fire数据集上IoU为22.50%。通过消融实验比较了各模块的独立贡献及其组合效果。

Conclusion: 各模块集成后形成高度鲁棒的框架，显著提升了在多样复杂环境条件下的分割一致性。未来工作将在大规模、多区域、不同卫星传感器的数据集上验证该架构，以建立其在全球野火检测中的更广泛泛化能力。

Abstract: Over the past decades, the frequency of global wildfires has been increasing steadily. Therefore, if the fire can be detected and precisely located at an early stage, the potential hazards caused by it can be minimized to the greatest extent. The machine learning methods based on satellite images, due to their ability to automatically monitor extremely remote and vast areas, have shown great potential for application in the field of wildfire detection. To address this challenge, we proposed a new model named spectral-morphological attention U-Net(SMA-UNet), which includes a spectral attention module, a residual attention UNet backbone, a channel-spatial modulator, and a pair of differentiable morphological gates. We trained and evaluated this model with two datasets. These modules, excluding the backbone, are used to detect active fire events for the first time, especially the pair of differentiable morphological gates, which is innovatively developed. The proposed model achieved the highest scores in both datasets (e.g., intersection over union 75.16% in TS-SatFire, 22.50% in Sen2Fire). By conducting ablation studies of each module, we compared their independent contributions and tested their combinations. Ultimately, the integration of these modules yields a highly robust framework that significantly improves segmentation consistency across diverse and complex environmental conditions. Future work will focus on validating the proposed architecture across large-scale, multi-regional datasets from different satellite sensors to establish its broader generalizability for global wildfire detection.

</details>


### [57] [WeedExpert-R1: Incentivizing Botanical Reasoning in MLLMs with Reinforcement Learning for Precision Weed Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16492)
*Zonglin Yang,Wei-Zhen Liang,Nevin Lawrence,Xin Qiao,Benjamin Riggan,Chi-En Chiang,Fuchen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WeedExpert-R1，一种通过可验证奖励学习视觉基础植物学推理的多模态大模型。该模型结合人工 curated 的植物性状词典与Auditor-Synthesizer工作流生成推理数据进行监督微调，并采用GRPO策略优化。在37个杂草物种、6个数据集上，WeedExpert-R1-4B以75.82%的精确集精度(IoU=0.5)、89.30%精确率和87.81%召回率，超越GPT-5.4、Gemini-3.1-Pro等专有模型及更大规模开源模型，并展现出开放词汇能力和对新物种的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统目标检测器使用封闭词汇集，无法跨区域部署，且无法在复杂农业场景中对预测结果进行解释。多模态大模型虽具备视觉定位和推理能力，但缺乏足够的植物学知识，导致细粒度杂草识别中出现幻觉。

Method: 采用领域特定的Chain-of-Thought合成管道，结合人工 curated 的植物性状词典与Auditor-Synthesizer LLM工作流生成推理数据用于监督微调；然后使用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) 基于格式、准确性、实例数量和响应长度等奖励信号进行优化。

Result: 在37个杂草物种、6个数据集上，WeedExpert-R1-4B达到75.82%精确集精度(IoU=0.5)、89.30%精确率和87.81%召回率，超越GPT-5.4、Gemini-3.1-Pro等专有模型及Qwen3-VL-30B-Instruct、Gemma-4-31B-it等更大开源模型。

Conclusion: 模型在未见物种上的表现证明了其开放词汇能力和跨区域、跨作物部署的潜力，无需重新训练即可适应不同场景。

Abstract: Precision weed control requires species-level identification and instance-level localization. However, conventional object detectors use a closed vocabulary, limiting their deployment across regions, and cannot explain their predictions in complex agricultural scenes. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) offer visual grounding and reasoning capabilities, but insufficient botanical knowledge can cause hallucinations in fine-grained weed identification. This study introduces WeedExpert-R1, a multimodal model that learns visually grounded botanical reasoning through verifiable rewards. A domain-specific Chain-of-Thought synthesis pipeline combines a human-curated botanical trait dictionary with an Auditor-Synthesizer LLM workflow to generate reasoning data for supervised fine-tuning. Group Relative Policy Optimization is then applied with rewards for format, accuracy, instance count, and response length. Across 37 weed species from six datasets, WeedExpert-R1-4B achieved 75.82 percent exact-set precision at an IoU threshold of 0.5, 89.30 percent precision, and 87.81 percent recall. It outperformed proprietary models, including GPT-5.4 and Gemini-3.1-Pro, and larger open-source models, including Qwen3-VL-30B-Instruct and Gemma-4-31B-it. Results on unseen species further demonstrate its open-vocabulary capability and potential for deployment across diverse regions and crops without retraining.

</details>


### [58] [Geometry-Enhanced Portion Estimation for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16514)
*Lin Liao,Peng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种增强多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的轻量级份量估计方法，通过几何增强网络和结构化体积预测，在不依赖深度传感器或微调MLLM的情况下，在三个真实世界基准上将每类食物的份量误差降低33-41%，超越了所有旗舰MLLM和原始基准模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图像的膳食评估虽前景广阔，但份量估计仍是主要瓶颈。现有的多模态大语言模型在零样本食物识别上表现优异，但在份量估计方面能力薄弱。

Method: 在冻结的商业MLLM基础上附加一个轻量份量估计头：基于冻结的DINOv2骨干网络，结合几何增强网络和结构化softmax-占有体积预测。该模块仅利用MLLM输出的食物名称、边界框和密度范围，无需深度传感器或对MLLM进行微调。

Result: 在三个真实世界开放词汇基准上，该方法将每类食物的份量估计误差相对于原始MLLM降低33-41%，超越了Gemini、GPT、Claude等所有旗舰MLLM的直接估计结果，并超过了每个基准上原始发表的纯图像模型在其自身报告指标上的表现。

Conclusion: 提出了一种轻量级、无需额外传感器或微调的方法，有效解决了MLLM在膳食份量估计上的短板，显著提升了开放场景下的定量评估能力。

Abstract: Image-based dietary assessment promises to replace costly, bias-prone manual recalls, but portion estimation remains a major blocker. Multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) recognize a wide range of foods zero-shot in uncontrolled photos, yet they are weak at portion estimation -- a gap we measure across the current frontier (Gemini, GPT, and Claude flagships alike). We present a method that enhances a frozen, commercial MLLM with an accurate portion head: a small geometry-enhanced network on a frozen DINOv2 backbone with a structured softmax-ownership volume, consuming the MLLM's per-food name, bounding box, and density range -- no depth sensor, no MLLM fine-tuning. Evaluated fully open-vocabulary on three real-world benchmarks, the head cuts per-food portion error by 33-41% relative to the MLLM alone, outperforms every flagship MLLM's direct estimates, and surpasses each benchmark's originally published image-only model at its own reported metric.

</details>


### [59] [Do Vision Encoders Exhibit Human-like Color Thresholds?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16540)
*Engy Ehab,Pablo Hernández-Cámara,Nahla Belal,Jesús Malo,Javier Vazquez-Corral,Alexandra Gomez-Villa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过对比50多种预训练视觉编码器（包括CNN和ViT）与人类色觉辨别阈值，发现这些模型在色觉敏感性上与人类存在显著差异（最佳mIoU<0.25），其中自监督编码器优于监督编码器，而语言监督模型表现最两极分化，表明当前大规模视觉训练目标无法自然产生类人色觉敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解人类颜色感知是一个长期研究目标，传统方法通过寻找颜色辨别阈值来刻画人类色觉。尽管深度视觉编码器已被广泛使用，但很少有研究探究它们的内部表征是否展现出类似人类的辨别阈值。

Method: 对50多种预训练视觉编码器（包括卷积网络和视觉Transformer）进行大规模探索性研究，使用受控色度刺激在多个色度水平上，通过区域重叠度量（mIoU）比较模型得出的色度辨别阈值与人类辨别椭圆。

Result: 所有模型家族与人类感知阈值的对齐普遍较弱，最佳mIoU<0.25。自监督编码器一致优于监督编码器，语言监督模型表现最两极分化，同时占据排名顶端和底端。

Conclusion: 类人色觉敏感性不会从当前大规模视觉训练目标中自然涌现，不论架构类型如何。

Abstract: Understanding and characterizing human color perception is a longstanding research goal. One of the most traditional approaches is looking for the human color discrimination thresholds, the minimum chromatic differences perceptible to human observers. In recent years, deep neural networks have become the standard networks for computer vision tasks. In particular, deep vision encoders, foundation models trained on large-scale visual data, map images into latent feature representations. Despite the widespread use of deep vision encoders, few studies have investigated whether their internal representations exhibit human-like discrimination thresholds. In this work, we present a large-scale exploratory study probing the chromatic sensitivity of more than 50 pretrained vision encoders, including convolutional networks and vision transformers, against human discrimination thresholds. Using controlled chromatic stimuli at multiple chroma levels, we compare model-derived chromatic discrimination thresholds with human discrimination ellipses through a region-overlap metric (mIoU). Our analysis reveals generally weak alignment between model representations and human perceptual thresholds across all model families, with the best mIoU < 0.25. Moreover, we find that self-supervised encoders consistently outperform supervised ones, while language-supervised models show the most polarized behavior, occupying both the top and bottom of the ranking. These findings suggest that human-like chromatic sensitivity does not emerge naturally from current large-scale visual training objectives for any of the analyzed architectures.

</details>


### [60] [AffectFuse: Cross-Task Feature Fusion with Temporal Modeling for Multi-Task Affective Behavior Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16546)
*Dipit Saha,Mohammad Raihan Rashid,Shah Mohammad Abdul Mannan,Ahnaf Tahmid,Md. Mehedi Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对ABAW竞赛MTL赛道，提出了一种基于后编码器适配的多任务情感识别系统，通过冻结AffectNet预训练骨干、任务特定时序头、跨任务融合模块及AU识别中的LoRA适配，在s-Aff-Wild2验证集上取得P=1.7302，证明无需训练新的大规模人脸基础模型即可实现强MTL流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外情感行为识别需要联合预测连续价-唤醒度、分类表情和多标签动作单元，现有方法往往需要训练新的大规模基础模型，计算成本高且泛化性受限。本文旨在探索无需重新训练基础模型的后编码器适配策略，实现高效的多任务学习。

Method: 采用冻结的AffectNet监督骨干提取多分辨率特征，为不同任务设计独立的时序头处理模块；引入跨任务融合模块在各任务间选择有用信号；AU识别部分使用MAE-Face并结合LoRA进行低秩适配，通过DISFA数据集上的逐单元专家路由替代直接顺序迁移。

Result: 在s-Aff-Wild2官方验证集上取得P=1.7302的性能指标。

Conclusion: 后编码器适配与任务特定建模选择能够构建强大的多任务学习流水线，无需训练新的大规模人脸基础模型即可在野外情感分析任务中取得竞争性表现。

Abstract: Affective behavior recognition in the wild requires joint prediction of continuous valence-arousal, categorical facial expression, and multi-label action units from unconstrained face images. We present our system for the Multi-Task Learning (MTL) track of the 11th Affective Behavior Analysis in-the-wild (ABAW) competition on s-Aff-Wild2, the static selected-frame version of Aff-Wild2. The method focuses on post-encoder adaptation: frozen AffectNet-supervised backbones provide multi-resolution features, while task-specific temporal heads and cross-task fusion modules select the useful signals for each target. For action-unit recognition, we adapt MAE-Face with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) and use DISFA through per-unit expert routing rather than direct sequential transfer. Ablations over backbone, temporal, fusion, and AU-adaptation choices define the final configuration. The final system obtains P = 1.7302 on the official validation split, showing that post-encoder adaptation and task-wise modeling choices provide a strong MTL pipeline without training a new large-scale face foundation model.

</details>


### [61] [CNS-Edit++: Category-Agnostic 3D Editing with Coupled Neural Shape Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16577)
*Jingyu Hu,Weilong Yan,Zhengzhe Liu,Haipeng Li,Ka-Hei Hui,Hao,Zhang,Chi-Wing Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CNS-Edit++框架，基于耦合神经形状（CNS）表示和神经特征体积优化，将类别特定的3D形状编辑泛化为类别无关的3D形状编辑，支持多种编辑操作（复制、缩放、删除、混合、点拖动、区域拖动），并引入KV-cache替换和潜在特征正则化两种区域控制机制，在多个3D生成模型上取得了优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D形状编辑方法大多局限于特定类别，缺乏通用性；同时需要一种既能捕获高层语义又能提供局部空间上下文的可编辑3D表示，以支持多样化的编辑操作。

Method: 1) 提出耦合神经形状（CNS）表示，将全局潜在编码（捕获高层语义）与3D神经特征体积（提供局部空间上下文）耦合；2) 设计耦合神经形状优化过程，联合优化两个组件以响应用户编辑操作；3) 在类别特定3D反演模型和类别无关3D基础模型上实例化框架；4) 提供多种编辑算子（复制、缩放、删除、混合、点拖动、区域拖动），每类编辑被公式化为引导CNS优化的目标函数；5) 引入KV-cache替换和潜在特征正则化两种互补的区域控制机制以保护编辑区域外的内容。

Result: 在多种3D生成模型上的大量定量和定性评估表明，该方法在性能上显著优于现有最先进解决方案。

Conclusion: CNS-Edit++通过将耦合神经表示从类别特定扩展到类别无关，结合多种编辑算子和区域保护机制，实现了通用且高质量的3D形状编辑。

Abstract: This paper presents a latent-space 3D shape editing framework built upon a coupled neural shape (CNS) representation and a neural feature volume optimization. This work extends CNS-Edit, built on Coupled Neural Shape optimization, to CNS-Edit++, by generalizing the category-specific coupled representation to category-agnostic 3D shape editing with foundation models. The Coupled Neural Shape (CNS) representation couples a global latent code that captures high-level shape semantics with a 3D neural feature volume that provides spatial context for local shape manipulation. Then we formulate a coupled neural shape optimization procedure that co-optimizes these two components subject to a given editing operation. Our framework can be instantiated on both the category-specific 3D inversion model and category-agnostic 3D foundation models. We provide various shape editing operators, including copy, resize, delete, mix, point-wise drag, and region-wise drag, each of which is formulated as an objective to guide the CNS optimization. To preserve regions outside the editing area, we further introduce two complementary region-wise control mechanisms, i.e., KV-cache replacement and latent feature regularization. Extensive quantitative and qualitative evaluations across different 3D generative models demonstrate the strong capabilities of our approach over state-of-the-art solutions.

</details>


### [62] [Digital measurement of droplet flame diameter in microgravity combustion images using Segment Anything Model 2 with automatic prompt selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16587)
*Minghui Xu,Chaoyi Zhou,Aaron P. Cecil,Xi Liu,Siyu Huang,Yuhao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AI（SAM2）的自动化火焰直径测量工作流，通过自动提示点生成、RANSAC圆拟合和视频记忆机制，在19,537张微重力液滴燃烧图像上达到96.9%的平均一致性，效率提升约229倍，为数字图像计量学提供了可重复、全自动的测量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微重力液滴燃烧中，火焰直径是关键参数，但传统方法因soot尾迹、模糊边界、腔室反射和液滴漂移而存在测量偏差和操作者依赖性，亟需一种自动、可重复的精确测量方法。

Method: 将自动提示点生成集成到Segment Anything Model 2（SAM2）中，结合基于RANSAC的圆拟合进行火焰直径提取；引入视频记忆机制以维持漂移液滴的时间一致性，利用RANSAC剔除soot尾迹等几何异常点。

Result: 在19,537张正庚烷、正癸烷和正辛烷液滴燃烧图像上验证，与人工参考测量相比平均相对一致性为96.9%，平均绝对百分比误差为3.1%，显著优于传统Hough圆检测；测量精度随液滴尺寸增大而提高；组合标准不确定度为8.54%，效率提升约229倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的SAM2工作流为具有挑战性的燃烧图像提供了可重复、全自动且具备计量学表征的火焰直径数字测量系统，支持高通量燃烧诊断，展示了AI分割可集成到定量测量工作流中用于数字化图像计量学。

Abstract: Flame diameter is a key measurable parameter in microgravity droplet combustion, but its extraction from self-illuminated frames remains difficult because soot tails, blurred luminous boundaries, chamber reflections, and droplet drift introduce substantial measurement bias and operator dependence. This work presents an AI-enabled digital measurement workflow for automated flame diameter from combustion images. The workflow integrates automatic prompt-point generation into Segment Anything Model 2, employing Random Sample Consensus (RANSAC)-based circle fitting. The automatic prompt strategy removes subjective manual point selection, while the video memory mechanism maintains temporal consistency for drifting droplets, and the RANSAC fitting rejects soot-tail pixels as geometric outliers.
  The method is validated by 19,537 flame images of n-heptane, n-decane, and n-octane droplets with varying initial diameters. Compared with manual-reference measurements, the proposed workflow achieves a mean relative agreement of 96.9%, a mean absolute percentage error of 3.1%, and substantially outperforms conventional Hough circle detection, which performed worse under the same evaluation conditions. The results also show that the measurement accuracy improves with increasing droplet size. The proposed workflow has a combined standard uncertainty of 8.54% and achieves approximately a 229-fold improvement in efficiency over manual measurement.
  These results demonstrate that the proposed SAM2-based workflow provides a reproducible, fully automated, and metrologically characterized digital measurement system for extracting flame diameter from challenging combustion images. The approach supports high-throughput combustion diagnostics and illustrates that AI-based segmentation can be integrated into quantitative measurement workflows for digitalized image-based metrology.

</details>


### [63] [PAVXploreRL: Physical-Action-Visual World Model Reinforcement Learning with Action Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16602)
*Han Wang,Zijun Wang,Shuoshuo Xue,Rui Cao,Fenjiao Cheng,Xiaodang Liang,Roy Ka-Wei Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PAVXploreRL框架，通过强化学习优化物理合理性、动作遵循性和视觉保真度（PAV三目标），并结合分布内轨迹与噪声驱动的分布外动作探索，无需配对视频监督，显著提升世界模型的动作泛化能力和评估可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于动作条件的世界模型主要依赖分布内动作-视频对和像素级重建损失，未能显式优化PAV目标，且在专家数据之外的分布外动作上泛化能力差。

Method: 基于预训练潜在世界模型构建强化学习框架，通过奖励驱动训练显式优化PAV目标；联合利用分布内轨迹和噪声驱动的分布外动作探索，无需配对视频监督。

Result: 在多个基准上平均提升5.6%，生成更高质量的PAV属性；作为策略评估器时提供更可靠的性能估计，减少了Ctrl-World等仅用专家数据的世界模型的过估计偏差。

Conclusion: PAVXploreRL通过强化学习显式优化PAV目标并引入分布外探索，有效提升了世界模型的动作泛化能力和评估可靠性，优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models are a key component of embodied AI, serving as scalable policy evaluators that reduce reliance on expensive real-world rollouts. To accurately capture diverse action-induced dynamics, such models should satisfy three key objectives-Physical Plausibility (P), Action Adherence (A), and Visual Fidelity (V), collectively referred to as PAV-while remaining robust to both in-distribution (ID) expert demonstrations and out-of-distribution (OOD) actions. However, existing methods primarily rely on ID action-video pairs and pixel-level reconstruction losses, which do not explicitly optimize PAV objectives and generalize poorly beyond expert data. To address this, we propose PAVXploreRL, a reinforcement learning framework built on a pretrained latent world model that explicitly optimizes PAV objectives through reward-driven training. To improve action generalization, our method jointly leverages ID trajectories and noise-driven OOD action exploration, without paired video supervision. Experiments show that PAVXploreRL consistently outperforms pretrained baselines, achieving a 5.6% average gain across benchmarks and producing higher-quality PAV properties. As a policy evaluator, it also yields more reliable performance estimates and reduces the overestimation bias of prior expert-only world models such as Ctrl-World. Code: https://github.com/Social-AI-Studio/PAVXploreRL

</details>


### [64] [Can Multimodal Large Language Models Understand OCT?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16609)
*Baochen Fu,Wenzhi Deng,Baihao Jin,Yang Li,Zihan Nie,Kailin Jiang,Yuntao Du,Weiye Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OCT-Bench是一个包含10,076道多选题、覆盖20项细粒度任务（感知/认知/推理三个维度）的OCT图像理解基准，系统评估了20个多模态大模型，发现现有模型在OCT可靠理解上仍有显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将OCT理解简化为粗粒度疾病分类或孤立的视觉问答，缺乏对从视觉感知到临床推理完整认知过程的充分评估。

Method: 基于7个公共数据集的4,137张OCT图像构建10,076道高质量多选题，按临床解读流程建立包含感知、认知、推理三个维度的20项细粒度任务分类体系，并系统评估20个代表性多模态大模型。

Result: 当前模型在OCT可靠理解上仍有显著差距，且医学领域适配或增大模型规模均未能在各能力层面上持续提升性能。

Conclusion: OCT-Bench为多模态大模型提供了全面细粒度的评估框架，有助于识别能力瓶颈并推动临床级别的OCT理解发展。

Abstract: Optical coherence tomography (OCT) imaging is essential for the diagnosis and treatment of retinal diseases. Although multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated considerable potential in medical image analysis, existing benchmarks largely reduce OCT understanding to coarse-grained disease classification or isolated visual question answering, leaving the complete cognitive process from visual perception to clinical reasoning insufficiently evaluated. To address this limitation, we introduce OCT-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark dedicated to OCT image understanding. OCT-Bench comprises 10,076 high-quality multiple-choice questions constructed from 4,137 OCT images across seven public datasets. Following the real-world clinical interpretation workflow, we establish a hierarchical capability taxonomy consisting of 20 fine-grained tasks across three dimensions: Perception, Cognition, and Reasoning. These tasks cover a broad range of capabilities, including imaging attributes, retinal anatomy, lesion characteristics, spatial relationships, disease assessment, therapeutic decision-making, and prognostic management. We systematically evaluate 20 representative MLLMs, including proprietary models, open-source general-purpose models, and medical-domain models. Experimental results demonstrate that current models remain substantially short of reliable OCT understanding. Moreover, neither medical-domain adaptation nor increased model scale consistently improves performance across capability levels. OCT-Bench enables comprehensive and fine-grained evaluation of MLLMs, providing a foundation for identifying capability bottlenecks and advancing clinically grounded OCT understanding.

</details>


### [65] [SPARE-GS: Structural Parsimony and Resource Efficiency for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16624)
*Zhang Chen,Shuai Wan,Fuzheng Yang,Jiazhi Xia,Weiyao Lin,Junhui Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPARE-GS通过全局预算约束优化框架，动态平衡3D高斯原语的空间分布与区域表示需求，在减少30.38%高斯数量和23.81%训练时间的同时提升PSNR，并增强下游压缩与剪枝性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS在训练效率和表示紧凑性上受限于过多的原语增殖，现有方法缺乏对原语空间分布的全局资源约束优化。

Method: 将结构演化形式化为全局预算约束优化问题，推导最优性条件（边际效用平衡）；提出SPARE-GS插件框架，通过估计容量归一化区域需求、分配自适应目标配额、利用区域预算偏差协调密集化/剪枝/自适应终止，实现结构分配的动态平衡。

Result: 在标准、加速及结构增强的3DGS流水线上，高斯数量平均减少30.38%，训练时间缩短23.81%，平均PSNR提升；紧凑表示改善下游处理速度及多种压缩/剪枝方法的率失真性能。

Conclusion: 全局结构预算调节策略具有广泛适用性，可有效提升3DGS的训练效率、表示紧凑性及下游任务性能。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves high-fidelity novel view synthesis in real-time; however its training efficiency and representation compactness are hindered by excessive primitive proliferation. To address this challenge, we formulate the structural evolution of 3DGS as a global budget-constrained optimization problem and derive an optimality condition, which requires the marginal utility of structural resources to be balanced across spatial regions under a finite primitive budget. Based on this formulation, we propose SPARE-GS, a general plug-and-play framework that dynamically aligns the distribution of 3D Gaussian primitives with regional representational demand. SPARE-GS estimates capacity-normalized regional demand, assigns adaptive target quotas, and uses regional budget deviations to coordinate densification, pruning and adaptive termination toward a more balanced structural allocation. Extensive experiments across standard, accelerated, and structure-enhanced 3DGS pipelines demonstrate that SPARE-GS reduces the Gaussian count and training time by an average of 30.38% and 23.81%, respectively, while improving the average PSNR. Moreover, the resulting compact representations reduce downstream processing time and improve the rate-distortion performance of diverse compression and pruning methods, demonstrating the broad applicability of global structural budget regulation.

</details>


### [66] [TellTale: Blending Multi-Instance LoRA Text Encoders and a Zero-Shot LLM Judge for Ambivalence/Hesitancy Recognition in Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16635)
*Abdel-Karim Al-Tamimi,Ali Rodan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TellTale是一个纯文本方法，通过结合两个微调文本编码器（MIL目标）和一个零样本提示的14B指令LLM的三流概率加权融合，在BAH数据集上实现了0.7364 Macro-F1和0.7940平均精度，显著优于官方视觉基线（0.2827 Macro-F1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在面试视频中仅使用文本转录进行矛盾/犹豫（A/H）识别的可行性，以简化多模态依赖并证明文本模态在此任务中的有效性。

Method: 提出TellTale方法，包含三个概率流：1) 两个文本编码器（multilingual-e5-large和mDeBERTa-v3-base）使用LoRA适配器进行参数高效微调，采用多实例学习（MIL）目标对转录块独立评分并通过平滑最大池化聚合；2) 一个无需训练的14B指令LLM零样本提示评分；3) 通过加权平均和单一决策阈值融合三流概率，超参数通过参与者分组的交叉验证选择。

Result: 在152个未见参与者的私有测试集上，Macro-F1为0.7364，平均精度为0.7940，显著优于官方视觉基线（Macro-F1 0.2827）。

Conclusion: 纯文本方法在A/H识别任务中可以取得优秀效果，远超过视觉基线，表明转录文本包含丰富的矛盾/犹豫信号，多模态并非必要。

Abstract: We present TellTale, a text-only approach to ambivalence/hesitancy (A/H) recognition in interview videos, evaluated on the BAH dataset as part of the 3rd A/H Video Recognition Challenge (11th ABAW Workshop, ECCV 2026). Although the dataset provides video, audio, facial crops, and transcripts, TellTale relies on the transcript alone and combines three probability streams. Two text encoders, multilingual-e5-large and mDeBERTa-v3-base, are fine-tuned with parameter-efficient LoRA adapters under a multiple-instance learning (MIL) objective, in which transcript chunks are scored individually and pooled with a smooth maximum so that only the video-level label is needed for supervision. The third stream requires no training: a quantized 14B instruction LLM is prompted, zero-shot, to rate each transcript for A/H. The three probabilities are combined by a weighted average and a single decision threshold, both selected on participant-grouped cross-validated predictions. On the organizer-scored private test set of 152 videos from unseen participants, TellTale achieves a Macro-F1 of 0.7364 and an average precision of 0.7940, compared with 0.2827 Macro-F1 for the official vision-based baseline.

</details>


### [67] [DARA: Degradation-Aware Low-Rank Residual Adaptation with Original-to-Corrupted Distillation for Corruption-Robust Animal Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16644)
*Cynthia Xie,Talia Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DARA方法，通过冻结预训练骨干网络、学习路由低秩残差专家来修复退化图像的特征嵌入，并利用原始-退化蒸馏保留个体身份信息，在多种动物重识别数据集上以极低参数和计算开销显著提升对模糊、噪声等视觉退化的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物重识别依赖的细粒度身份线索易受模糊、噪声、压缩等视觉退化干扰，现有基于退化增强训练或像素级修复的方法仅间接提升鲁棒性，未能显式修复身份检索空间中的特征偏移。

Method: DARA将鲁棒动物重识别建模为输入条件化的特征空间修复问题：冻结微调后的骨干网络，学习路由低秩残差专家以自适应调整退化输入嵌入（无需退化类型标注）；同时引入原始-退化蒸馏损失，利用原始图像教师保持个体嵌入和检索关系。

Result: 在ATRW、FriesianCattle2017、MPDD、SeaStarReID2023四个数据集上，DARA相比标准和基于增强的微调显著提升了退化查询检索性能，能泛化到未见退化和跨域评估，恢复了退化查询mAP与完全退化微调之间差距的77.0%，仅增加0.49%参数和0.05%FLOPs。

Conclusion: DARA是一种轻量高效的后处理方法，通过显式修复特征空间中的退化偏移显著提升动物重识别模型的鲁棒性，且无需退化类型标注，参数和计算开销极小。

Abstract: Animal re-identification (Re-ID) relies on fine-grained identity cues that can be disrupted by blur, noise, compression, and other visual degradations. Existing robustness strategies based on degradation-augmented training or pixel-level restoration improve robustness indirectly, but do not explicitly repair shifts in the identity retrieval space. We study corruption-robust animal Re-ID as input-conditioned feature-space repair and introduce DARA, a lightweight retrofit for compact Re-ID models. DARA freezes the fine-tuned backbone and learns routed low-rank residual experts to adapt degraded-input embeddings without corruption-type annotations. To stabilize this adaptive repair, original-to-corrupted distillation uses an original-image teacher to preserve individual embeddings and retrieval relations. Experiments on ATRW, FriesianCattle2017, MPDD, and SeaStarReID2023 show that DARA improves corrupted-query retrieval over standard and augmentation-based fine-tuning, generalizes to unseen corruptions and cross-domain evaluation, and recovers 77.0% of the corrupted-query mAP gap to full corrupted fine-tuning while adding only 0.49% parameters and 0.05% FLOPs.

</details>


### [68] [DRIFT: Difficulty-aware Rectified Flows for Through-plane MRI Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16649)
*Yoonseok Choi,Eun-Gyu Ha,Daniel Kim,Mohammed A. Al-masni,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Dong-Hyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DRIFT提出了一种两阶段厚度条件整流流框架，用于连续输入切片厚度的贯通平面MRI超分辨率。第一阶段用解剖投影网络生成粗分辨率初始化，第二阶段用整流流细化细节，并通过物理感知难度度量和自适应积分调度器降低推理成本，同时引入一致性端点轨迹对齐损失保证厚度一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI各向异性分辨率导致贯通平面存在阶梯伪影，现有超分辨率方法面临效率-保真度权衡：前馈回归器速度快但在大切片厚度下过度平滑，采样方法保真度高但推理成本大。

Method: 提出DRIFT框架，包含两阶段：Stage 1使用解剖投影网络(APN)将低分辨率块映射到粗高分辨率流形；Stage 2通过整流流细化细节，引入物理感知难度(PAD)指标引导自适应积分调度器(AIS)，并采用一致性端点轨迹对齐(CETA)损失。

Result: DRIFT在超分辨率基线方法上表现更优，同时降低了推理成本。

Conclusion: DRIFT通过两阶段设计有效平衡了效率与保真度，为连续切片厚度的贯通平面MRI超分辨率提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is often acquired with anisotropic resolution to reduce scan time, producing stair-step artifacts along the through-plane direction. In through-plane MRI super-resolution, an efficiency-fidelity trade-off arises: feed-forward regressors are fast but oversmooth at large slice-thicknesses, while sampling-based methods improve fidelity at high inference cost. We propose DRIFT, a two-stage thickness-conditioned rectified flow framework for through-plane MRI super-resolution with continuous input slice-thickness. Stage 1 employs an Anatomical Projection Network (APN) to map low-resolution patches to a coarse high-resolution manifold, providing a deterministic anatomical initialization that shortens the residual transport of Stage 2 and stabilizes slice-wise refinement. Stage 2 refines details via rectified flow and introduces a Physics-Aware Difficulty (PAD) metric derived from slice-thickness induced through-plane bandwidth deficit to guide an Adaptive Integration Scheduler (AIS), allocating ODE steps by thickness. A Consistent Endpoint Trajectory Alignment (CETA) loss enforces thickness-consistent reconstructions. Experiments show that DRIFT outperforms super-resolution baselines while reducing inference cost. Code, models, and interactive demos are available at https://yoonseokchoi-ai.github.io/drift-eccv2026/.

</details>


### [69] [Position: Explanation Stability Is a Property of the Model Method Pair, Not the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16652)
*Kabilan Elangovan,Daniel Ting*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文论证了解释稳定性必须通过跨方法验证才具有科学有效性。实验表明，不同归因方法下模型稳定性排名完全相反，说明稳定性是模型-方法对的涌现属性，而非模型固有特性。因此，基于解释的声明应通过多种归因方法验证，监管提交需明确指定归因算子以避免虚假安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对模型解释的稳定性缺乏统一的验证标准，论文试图论证仅依赖单一归因方法得出的稳定性结论在科学上是无效的，并揭示稳定性是模型与归因方法联合作用的涌现属性。

Method: 采用Position Paper论证方式，结合胸部X光片对照实验：使用DenseNet201、ResNet50V2和InceptionV3三个模型（AUC均>99%），通过LayerCAM和GradCAM++两种归因方法进行稳定性排名对比分析。

Result: LayerCAM排名中InceptionV3最稳定（IoU=0.777），而GradCAM++则显示DenseNet201最优，InceptionV3稳定性下降17.3%。模型稳定性排名在不同归因方法间完全逆转。

Conclusion: 解释稳定性不是模型的固有属性，而是模型与归因方法的配对属性。基于解释的声明应跨多种归因方法验证，监管提交须明确指定所采用的归因算子，避免因单一方法导致的虚假安全保证。

Abstract: This position paper argues that claims about explanation stability are scientifically invalid without cross method validation.
