Abstract: Continuous diffusion models have become the dominant paradigm for photo-realistic image Super-Resolution (SR), but they typically formulate reconstruction as continuous signal-level denoising and incorporate semantic priors through external conditioning modules. This makes it less direct to exploit the unified token-based scaling paradigm of modern multimodal models. Autoregressive models provide a more native semantic representation by modeling images as discrete visual tokens, yet their causal decoding is inefficient for high-resolution reconstruction. Discrete diffusion offers a promising middle ground by enabling non-causal, parallel prediction over visual tokens. However, directly adapting discrete diffusion to SR remains non-trivial due to two task-specific challenges: (1) the long-tailed distribution of visual tokens, which under-represents rare but perceptually critical textures; and (2) spatially inconsistent parallel decoding, which may introduce isolated artifacts. To address these issues, we propose DiMOO-SR, a rarity-aware multimodal discrete diffusion framework for photo-realistic image SR. During training, Inverse Frequency Sampling (IFS) prioritizes under-represented but information-rich tokens. During inference, Spatial Consistency Ranking (SCR) refines token confidence using local neighborhood agreement to improve structural coherence. Extensive experiments on widely used real-world SR benchmarks demonstrate that DiMOO-SR achieves competitive perceptual quality with only a few parallel decoding steps, highlighting the potential of discrete diffusion for generative image super-resolution. The code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [140] [Brain-Aligned Multi-Stream Video Transformers with Sparse Self-Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17625)
*Amir Hosein Fadaei,Mahyar Maleki,Mohammad-Reza A. Dehaqani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经启发式的分裂融合视频Transformer，用稀疏赢家通吃模块替代密集自注意力，并设计了高分辨率低帧率的"什么"流和低分辨率高帧率的"哪里"流两条互补通路。在Kinetics-400和Something-Something V2上实现了精度与推理时间的帕累托最优，并且通过EEG表征相似性分析达到了0.18的脑-模型相关性（约78%噪声天花板），优于强视频Transformer基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频Transformer通常忽略灵长类视觉原理，也极少用神经数据评估，缺乏生物可解释性。作者希望通过引入灵长类视觉中的竞争路由和双通路分离机制，构建兼具效率和脑对齐能力的视频理解模型。

Method: 1) 提出稀疏赢家通吃（winner-takes-all）令牌选择模块替代密集自注意力，模拟生物视觉回路中的竞争路由。2) 设计分裂融合（split-and-fuse）架构，包含两条互补通路：高分辨率低帧率的"什么"流（what stream）和低分辨率高帧率的"哪里"流（where stream），在分类前融合。3) 使用时序分辨EEG记录进行表征相似性分析评估脑-模型对齐度。

Result: 在Kinetics-400和Something-Something V2上，最佳变体在同等规模和预训练条件下处于精度与推理时间的帕累托前沿，并对空间扰动具有更好的鲁棒性。脑-模型相关性峰值达到0.18（约78%噪声天花板）持续优于强视频Transformer基线。

Conclusion: 通路特化和稀疏竞争是高效且与脑对齐的视频理解的有效归纳偏置，证实了灵长类视觉原理对视频Transformer设计的实用价值。

Abstract: Modern video transformers typically ignore principles from primate vision and are rarely evaluated against neural data, limiting their biological interpretability. We introduce a sparse winner-takes-all token selection module that replaces dense self-attention to improve efficiency and approximate competitive routing observed in biological visual circuits. We further propose a neuro-inspired split-and-fuse video transformer which uses two complementary pathways: a high-resolution, low-frame-rate "what" stream and a low-resolution, high-frame-rate "where" stream, fused before classification. On Kinetics-400 and Something-Something V2, our best variant operates on the Pareto frontier of accuracy versus inference time among models of comparable scale and pretraining, and showing improved robustness to spatial perturbations. Using representational similarity analysis between model embeddings and time-resolved EEG recordings for the same video stimuli, our model attains a peak brain-model correlation of 0.18 (about 78% of the noise ceiling) and consistently outperforms strong video transformer baselines, suggesting that pathway specialization and sparse competition are useful inductive biases for efficient, brain-aligned video understanding.

</details>


### [141] [MixDiffusion: Mixing Diffusion-based Uni-condition Text-to-Image Generation Models for Multi-condition Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17634)
*Pengcheng Wan,Liang Han,Lin Xu,Bowen Xiao,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MixDiffusion 提出了一种无需训练的扩散框架，通过整合多个预训练的单条件扩散模型，理论上支持任意数量的控制条件（如边界框、关键点、草图、深度图、参考图像和文本）进行多条件文本到图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的文本到图像生成方法大多局限于单一类型的控制条件（如边界框或关键点），灵活性受限。MixDiffusion 旨在解决多条件控制的局限性。

Method: 通过推导出的积分公式，在扩散模型的每个去噪步骤中，从多个单条件扩散模型的预测噪声分布中联合推导出多条件图像生成模型的预测噪声分布，并提供了严格的理论证明。该方法无需训练，易于部署和扩展。

Result: MixDiffusion 理论上支持任意数量的控制条件，包括边界框、关键点、草图、深度图、参考图像和文本，并能有效整合多种预训练的单条件扩散模型。

Conclusion: MixDiffusion 是一种训练免费、易于部署且易于扩展到新控制模态的多条件文本到图像生成框架，克服了现有方法仅支持单一控制条件的限制。

Abstract: Recent advances in text-to-image (T2I) generation have enabled controllable image synthesis by incorporating conditions beyond text. However, most existing diffusion-based methods are limited to a single type of control condition (e.g., bounding boxes or keypoints), which restricts their flexibility. To address this limitation, we propose MixDiffusion, a training-free diffusion framework for multi-condition T2I generation. MixDiffusion theoretically supports an arbitrary number of control conditions, including bounding boxes, keypoints, sketches, depth maps, reference images, and text, by collaboratively integrating multiple pre-trained uni-condition diffusion models. The key insight of the proposed approach is to derive the predicted noise distribution in each denoising step of the diffusion-based multi-condition image generation model from the predicted noise distributions of multiple diffusion-based uni-condition models with a derived integration formula, which is supported by rigorous theory proof. Owing to its training-free nature, MixDiffusion is easy to deploy and readily extensible to new control modalities.

</details>


### [142] [Reviving Ancient Paintings via Poem: A Colorization Framework for Aligning Cultural Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17638)
*Junming Gao,Biao Zhu,Xiaosong Wang,Tan Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PoemColor框架，通过诗画投影器(P3)和结构感知语义注意力(SASA)实现古诗引导的古画着色，解决颜色模糊、语义偏差和颜色泄漏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古画褪色破坏了"诗画合一"的美学原则，现有扩散模型存在视觉恢复模糊、文本引导带来现代语义偏差、过饱和及跨边界颜色泄漏等问题。

Method: 1) Poetic Painting Projector (P3)：通过诗到调色板预训练将诗歌语义转化为古典颜色感知条件；2) Structure-Aware Semantic Attention (SASA)：联合控制诗歌颜色语义的传播方向和区域注入强度；3) 构建混合修复数据集，融合合成退化与专家修复样本。

Result: 在多项实验上显著优于现有方法，实现了兼具历史真实性和诗歌语义的可控着色。

Conclusion: PoemColor有效解决了古诗引导的古画着色中的语义对齐和颜色控制问题，为文化遗产数字化修复提供了新思路。

Abstract: The irreversible fading of ancient paintings disrupts the "congruence between poems and paintings", a core aesthetic principle where visual imagery harmonizes with literary inscriptions. Although diffusion models provide strong generative priors, restoring historically faithful colors remains difficult: visual restoration is inherently ambiguous, while direct text guidance often causes modern semantic bias, over-saturation, and cross-boundary color leakage. To address this, we propose PoemColor, a poem-guided ancient painting colorization framework. Our method aligns poetic cultural semantics with painting restoration through two key designs. First, the Poetic Painting Projector (P3) converts implicit poetic context into a classical color-aware condition via poem-to-palette pretraining, reducing the ambiguity of poem-to-color mapping. Second, Structure-Aware Semantic Attention (SASA) regulates how poetic color semantics are injected into the diffusion backbone by jointly controlling their propagation direction and regional injection strength. In addition, we construct a hybrid restoration dataset that integrates synthetic degradation with expert-restored artifacts, providing both scalable supervision and real classical color references. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods, delivering controllable colorization that revives both historical authenticity and poetic semantics.

</details>


### [143] [Direct Clinical Joint Angle Extraction from Parametric Body Model Rotation Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17639)
*Joey Páolo Kardolus,Daan Hendriks,Jaap Jansen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种直接从参数化人体模型（如GEM-X、SAM 3D Body）的每段旋转矩阵中读取临床关节角度的方法，无需逆运动学或肌肉骨骼模型拟合。在OpenCap LabValidation数据集上，单智能手机视频的关节角度平均绝对误差为4.50度（GEM-X）和4.66度（SAM 3D Body），与OpenCap Monocular的4.8度精度相当，但流程更简单。该方法无需参与者身高、相机内参或个体模型缩放，可实时运行，适用于居家、远程康复和大规模分散研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床关节角度测量工具通常缓慢、昂贵或局限于实验室环境，难以在常规诊疗中广泛应用。现有方法需要逆运动学或肌肉骨骼模型拟合，流程复杂且依赖多种输入（如身高、相机内参、个体模型缩放）。本文旨在开发一种更简单、低成本且可直接从视频中获取临床关节角度的方法，以拓宽运动分析的实用场景。

Method: 利用参数化人体模型（GEM-X、SAM 3D Body）已生成的每段旋转矩阵，通过一个小型校准表（而非优化过程）直接读取临床关节角度。方法无需逆运动学或肌肉骨骼模型拟合，仅需单智能手机视频作为输入，不依赖参与者身高、相机内参数据库或个体模型缩放。

Result: 在OpenCap LabValidation数据集上，GEM-X模型在15个关节角度的平均绝对误差为4.50度，SAM 3D Body为4.66度，与OpenCap Monocular的4.8度精度相当且统计上无显著差异。该方法可实时运行，且校准表可移植到其他人体模型。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法简化了临床关节角度测量流程，精度与现有方法相当，但显著降低了对硬件和先验信息的要求。这使运动分析能够从实验室拓展到临床、居家、远程康复和大规模分散研究等更广泛的实际应用场景。

Abstract: Quantitative joint angles are rarely available in routine care because the tools are slow, costly, or confined to a laboratory. We show that clinical joint angles can be read directly from the per-segment rotation matrices a parametric body model already produces, with no inverse-kinematics or musculoskeletal-model fitting step. On the OpenCap LabValidation cohort, using the GEM-X body-model estimator on single-smartphone video, our pooled mean absolute error is 4.50 degrees over the fifteen joint angles that match the OpenCap Monocular reference set, the same accuracy range as OpenCap Monocular's 4.8 degrees on the same cohort and reference standard, from a much simpler pipeline. The step that connects a body model to clinical angles is a small calibration table rather than an optimisation, so the same procedure transfers unchanged to other body models: repeating it on SAM 3D Body, changing only the table, gives 4.66 degrees, statistically indistinguishable from GEM-X, and runs in real time from a live single-camera stream. The method needs no per-recording inputs beyond the video itself: no participant height, no camera-intrinsics database, no per-subject model scaling. This broadens where movement analysis is practical, from in-clinic and at-home recording to telerehabilitation and large-scale decentralised studies.

</details>


### [144] [BMFA: Boundary-Minority Free-Energy Adaptive Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17656)
*Wenyan Xu,Alizer Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现Vision Transformer中因空间上小而高响应的边界少数区域被块均值低估，导致自由能估计偏差；提出BMFA方法，通过层次化分段常数近似和基于局部自由能下界增量的递归细化来筛选关键token，在多种实验上验证了有效性，显著降低低估误差并保持较高分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformer处理空间冗余token时，粗粒度token汇总可能丢失指数注意力聚合所需的证据，导致边界少数区域（空间小但高响应）被块均值严重低估，影响模型性能。

Method: 提出边界少数自由能自适应筛选（BMFA），构建层次化分段常数近似，根据可计算的局部自由能下界增量递归细化块，从而识别和保留边界少数区域的关键token。

Result: 合成测试中平均低估误差从2.582降至0.261（叶节点比5.794%）；COCO图像边缘平均差距从2.254降至0.526；ImageNet-1K上在55.861%叶节点比下保持71.520% Top-1准确率。

Conclusion: BMFA有效缓解了边界少数低估问题，在保留关键信息的同时大幅减少token数量，为后续稀疏核加速提供理论基础，但当前原型在完整QK计算后评估筛选质量，叶节点比表征的是表示粒度而非已验证的稀疏核加速。

Abstract: Vision Transformers process spatially redundant tokens efficiently only when coarse token summaries preserve the evidence required by exponential attention aggregation. We identify a boundary-minority underestimation failure in which a spatially small, high-response region contributes dominant Gibbs mass while remaining nearly invisible to a block mean. We formalize the failure through the discrepancy between normalized log-mean-exp free energy and mean summarization, prove that minority Gibbs mass can remain non-vanishing as its spatial support and mean contribution vanish, and characterize the limitations of finite-order moment corrections. Building on the resulting analysis, we introduce Boundary-Minority Free-Energy Adaptive Screening (BMFA), which constructs a hierarchical piecewise-constant approximation and recursively refines blocks according to a computable lower-bound increment of local free energy. Controlled synthetic tests, COCO and LVIS diagnostic probes, closed-loop DeiT-Tiny evaluations, and ImageNet-1K experiments establish a consistent evidence chain. BMFA reduces the mean synthetic underestimate from 2.582 to 0.261 at a 5.794% leaf ratio, lowers the COCO image-edge mean gap from 2.254 to 0.526, and preserves 71.520% ImageNet Top-1 accuracy at a 55.861% leaf ratio. The current prototype evaluates selection quality after full QK computation; the reported leaf ratio therefore characterizes representation granularity rather than verified sparse-kernel speedup.

</details>


### [145] [RayOcc: Occlusion-Aware Ray Occupancy Estimation via Gaussian Mixture Intensity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17660)
*Junho Kim,Seongwon Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RayOcc提出了一种基于射线的多标签存在预测框架，通过非归一化高斯混合强度和泊松事件建模，将射线建模从单深度估计转化为多标签占用预测，有效处理了深度模糊和遮挡问题，在nuScenes上达到了基于高斯方法的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机-only 3D语义占用预测方法沿单条射线只预测单一深度假设，无法处理多条空间分离表面共存的遮挡场景，忽视了占用预测本质上是一个多标签存在性问题。

Method: 1) 将每条射线的建模转化为多标签存在预测，估计非归一化高斯混合强度；2) 通过泊松事件公式将混合强度转换为区间占用概率，允许多个占用假设共存且不强制竞争；3) 用预测的混合分量初始化稀疏3D高斯原语，经精炼和光栅化得到语义占用预测。

Result: 在nuScenes基准上，RayOcc在整体IoU和mIoU上均达到基于高斯方法的SOTA。

Conclusion: 将射线建模从单深度估计重新定义为多标签存在预测，可有效提升复杂遮挡场景下的3D语义占用预测性能。

Abstract: Camera-only 3D semantic occupancy prediction aims to infer voxel-wise scene semantics from multi-view images, yet remains fundamentally challenging due to depth ambiguity and occlusion. Along a single camera ray, multiple spatially separated surfaces may coexist, making occupancy inherently a multi-label existence problem rather than a single-depth estimation task. However, most existing approaches favor a single dominant depth hypothesis per ray, limiting their ability to model volumetric scenes under complex occlusion. To address this limitation, we introduce RayOcc, an occlusion-aware ray occupancy framework that reformulates ray modeling as multi-label existence prediction. Instead of predicting a categorical depth distribution, RayOcc estimates a non-normalized Gaussian mixture intensity along each ray and converts it into interval-wise occupancy probabilities via a Poisson event formulation, allowing multiple occupied hypotheses to coexist without enforcing mutual competition across depth. The predicted mixture components are interpreted as occupancy hypotheses to initialize sparse 3D Gaussian primitives, which are refined and rasterized for semantic occupancy prediction. Experiments on the nuScenes benchmark show that RayOcc achieves state-of-the-art overall IoU and mIoU among the compared Gaussian-based occupancy methods.

</details>


### [146] [Early Yield Prediction for Sugar Beet Fields using Satellite Data -- Learnings from Specialized Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17661)
*Philipp Vaeth,Bhumika Laxman Sadbhave,Denise Dejon,Gunther Schorcht,Magda Gregorova*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究展示了如何将领域知识与机器学习紧密结合，利用纯光学Sentinel-2影像进行甜菜收获产量早期预测。通过使用极小视觉Transformer块大小和全部光谱波段，以及改进的训练设置和基于排名的低产田识别方法，实现了协同增益，能够在生长周期早期识别大量低产田块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管公开卫星影像在农业监测中越来越有价值，但如何有效将领域知识融入机器学习方法仍是一个挑战。本研究旨在通过一个真实的甜菜产量预测案例，探索领域知识与机器学习的深度融合路径。

Method: 采用极小的视觉Transformer（ViT）块大小并利用所有可用的Sentinel-2光谱波段进行模型训练；同时设计改进的训练流程，结合基于排名的低产田块检测方法，实现早期产量预测。

Result: 极小ViT块大小和全光谱波段的使用虽然在该领域不常见，但显著提升了模型性能；改进的训练方法使得能够在生长周期早期识别出大量低产田块。

Conclusion: 领域知识与机器学习的紧密整合可以产生协同增益，即使采用不符合领域常规的设计选择也能有效提升预测能力，为早期农业产量预测提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Remote sensing has become an increasingly valuable tool for agricultural monitoring, particularly through the use of publicly available satellite imagery. However, effectively integrating domain knowledge into machine learning methods remains challenging. This study presents a real-world example of early sugar beet harvest yield forecasting from purely optical Sentinel-2 imagery, demonstrating how a tight integration of domain knowledge and machine learning can lead to synergistic gains. We empirically find that using very small vision transformer patch sizes and all available Sentinel-2 spectral bands improves our model despite being uncommon design choices in the domain. As a practical contribution, we were able to identify a large fraction of low-yield fields in a different year early on in the growth cycle through a modified training setup and a ranking-based detection of underperforming fields.

</details>


### [147] [Attention from Above: A Multimodal Model for Drone-Based Object Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17669)
*Hyun-Ki Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于YOLO-World框架的改进多模态目标检测模型，通过将YOLOv8骨干网络中的C2f层替换为基于注意力机制的A2C2f层，显著提升了对小目标和边界清晰目标的检测性能。在VisDrone数据集上的实验表明，该模型在精度、召回率、F1分数和mAP等指标上均优于原始YOLO-World模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机目标检测技术已从预定义目标检测扩展到基于文本提示的指定目标检测，现有方法在处理小目标检测时性能不足，亟需一种高效的多模态检测模型来提升小目标的检测精度。

Method: 基于YOLO-World框架构建，将YOLOv8骨干网络中的C2f层替换为基于注意力机制的A2C2f层，通过注意力机制和并行处理结构增强局部特征的表示能力，特别是针对小目标和边界清晰的目标。

Result: 在VisDrone数据集上，相比原始YOLO-World模型，精度从43.0%提升至45.1%，召回率从32.8%提升至35.0%，F1分数从37.2%提升至39.4%，mAP@0.5从32.5%提升至35.2%，mAP@0.5-0.95从18.5%提升至19.9%。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法为无人机图像和视频应用场景中的目标检测提供了一种有效且高精度的解决方案。

Abstract: Drone-based object detection technology has advanced rapidly, becoming increasingly sophisticated and efficient. Recently, research trends have expanded beyond the detection of predefined objects toward the identification of specified target objects. For example, desired targets can be specified through textual prompts, enabling accurate detection of objects of interest. To address this demand, this paper proposes an efficient multimodal-based object detection model aimed at improving small object detection performance. The proposed method is built upon the YOLO-World framework and replaces the C2f layers used in the YOLOv8 backbone with attention-based A2C2f layers. This modification enables more precise representation of local features, particularly for small objects or objects with well-defined boundaries. In addition, the incorporation of attention mechanisms and parallel processing structures significantly enhances the model's computational accuracy. Comparative experiments conducted on the VisDrone dataset demonstrate that the proposed model outperforms the original YOLO-World model. Specifically, precision increases from 43.0% to 45.1%, recall from 32.8% to 35.0%, the F1 score from 37.2% to 39.4%, mAP@0.5 from 32.5% to 35.2%, and mAP@0.5-0.95 from 18.5% to 19.9%, confirming a substantial improvement in detection accuracy. These results verify that the proposed approach provides an effective and highly accurate solution for object detection in drone-based image and video application environments.

</details>


### [148] [ShotPlan: Cinematic Video Generation with Learnable Planning Token](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17675)
*Su Guo,Guangce Liu,Haosen Yang,Jiepeng Wang,Cong Liu,Junqi Liu,Haibin Huang,Hongxun Yao,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ShotPlan框架，通过可学习的规划令牌和分数时域旋转位置嵌入（FRoPE），在视频扩散基础模型上实现显式的多镜头电影化视频生成，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型在单次生成中表现优异，但在需要连贯叙事和有效多镜头构图的电影化视频生成中仍存在局限，缺乏显式的镜头规划机制。

Method: 提出ShotPlan框架，引入可学习的规划令牌（planning tokens）来捕捉镜头级转换线索，并与原始视频生成令牌无缝集成以控制转换时间戳；同时提出分数时域旋转位置嵌入（FRoPE），使镜头转换能够在帧级别进行建模。

Result: 实验表明，ShotPlan在电影化视频生成任务上显著优于现有方法，具有更灵活的镜头管理和更强的镜头间一致性。

Conclusion: 可学习的规划令牌结合FRoPE能够有效建模多镜头之间的过渡，提升电影化视频生成的叙事连贯性和镜头组合质量。

Abstract: Current video generation models achieve impressive results in single-shot generation, yet remain limited in cinematic video generation, where coherent narratives and effective multi-shot composition require explicit shot planning. To address this challenge, we propose ShotPlan, a framework for explicit multi-shot cinematic video generation built upon a video diffusion foundation model. Our method introduces learnable planning tokens that capture shot-level transition cues and can be seamlessly integrated with the original video generation tokens to control transition timestamps. Unlike standard video generation tokens, the proposed planning tokens are equipped with Fractional Temporal Rotary Position Embedding (FRoPE), enabling shot transitions to be modeled at the frame level. Experiments demonstrate that ShotPlan significantly outperforms existing cinematic video generation methods, offering more flexible shot management and stronger inter-shot consistency.

</details>


### [149] [Memory-Supported Synergistic Adaptation for Training-Free Test-Time Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17693)
*Lingrui Li,Nan Pu,Dong Zhao,Wenjing Li,Andrew P French,Zhun Zhong,Xin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MSSA，一种无需训练的无参数自适应框架，通过在线记忆库和跨图像结构对齐，有效提升视觉语言模型在医学图像分割中的测试时自适应性能，在多个基准上取得最高12.2% DSC和11.7% mIoU的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时自适应方法在医学图像分割中面临挑战：基于VLM生成预测的优化带来的增益往往被噪声驱动学习对VLM预训练特征的破坏所抵消，导致改进有限且不稳定。因此需要一种能避免参数更新、同时有效利用VLM跨模态能力的自适应方法。

Method: MSSA包含两个核心模块：(i) 噪声感知记忆构建模块，用于筛选和稳定跨模态预测，构建在线记忆库；(ii) 相关性驱动原型对齐模块，将目标样本与结构一致的记忆样本及其可靠预测进行对齐，无需更新模型参数即可实现鲁棒自适应。

Result: 在多个医学图像分割基准上，MSSA一致提升VLM分割模型性能，显著优于现有基于微调的TTA方法，最高提升12.2% DSC和11.7% mIoU。

Conclusion: MSSA通过内存支持和协同对齐策略，以无需训练的方式有效解决了VLM在医学图像分割中自适应不稳定的问题，为测试时自适应提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) aims to mitigate distribution shifts by adapting models with unlabeled target data at inference time. While TTA with vision-language models (VLMs) has shown promising results in classification, extending it to medical image segmentation remains challenging. In this setting, the adaptation gains from optimizing on VLM-generated predictions are often outweighed by the degradation to the VLM's strong pretrained features caused by noisy, update-driven learning, resulting in limited and unstable improvements. We therefore propose Memory-Supported Synergistic Adaptation (MSSA), a novel training-free TTA framework for medical image segmentation. Without updating model parameters, MSSA dynamically selects reliable image-text predictions to construct an online memory, uses them as text-guided semantic priors, and couples them with cross-image structural alignment for robust adaptation. Specifically, MSSA consists of (i) a noise-aware memory construction module that filters and stabilizes cross-modal predictions, and (ii) a relevance-driven prototype alignment module that aligns the target sample with structurally consistent memory samples and their reliable predictions to improve adaptation. Extensive experiments on multiple medical segmentation benchmarks demonstrate that MSSA consistently improves VLM-based segmentation models and outperforms existing fine-tuning-based TTA methods by a clear margin, with gains of up to 12.2% DSC and 11.7% mIoU. Project page: https://lingrayy.github.io/MSSA/ .

</details>


### [150] [PC-Seg: Progressive Cross-View Consistency for 3D OCT Segmentation from Sparse 2D Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17718)
*Tsubasa Konno,Takahiro Ninomiya,Yukun Zhou,Koichi Ito,Siegfried K. Wagner,Yiqun Lin,Pearse A. Keane,Toru Nakazawa,Takafumi Aoki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PC-Seg提出了一种渐进式交叉视图一致性框架，利用稀疏2D标注（约0.7%训练数据）实现高精度3D OCT图像分割，通过单2D模型学习交叉视图一致性生成伪标签，再蒸馏至3D模型并进行协同训练，在MSHC和Duke DME数据集上达到与全监督方法相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCT图像体积分割需要大量体素级标注，成本高昂；现有半监督方法逐切片处理，未能充分利用3D空间上下文信息。

Method: 1) 单2D模型从标准B扫描和正交切片中学习交叉视图一致性，生成可靠的3D伪标签；2) 将伪标签蒸馏到3D模型；3) 2D与3D模型通过集成伪标签进行协同训练，相互精炼。

Result: 在MSHC和Duke DME数据集上，仅使用约0.7%训练数据的标签，PC-Seg精度即可与全监督学习相媲美，并优于现有半监督和视网膜层分割方法。

Conclusion: PC-Seg有效解决了稀疏标注下OCT图像3D分割的挑战，证明了交叉视图一致性和模型协同训练策略在大幅降低标注成本的同时保持高精度分割的可行性。

Abstract: Volumetric segmentation of optical coherence tomography (OCT) images is essential for diagnosing ocular diseases but requires labor-intensive voxel-wise annotations. While semi-supervised learning (SSL) can reduce annotation costs, most existing methods process data slice by slice and fail to exploit the inherent 3D spatial context. We propose PC-Seg, a progressive cross-view consistency framework that learns high-accuracy 3D segmentation models from sparse 2D annotations. Unlike conventional multi-view approaches, PC-Seg uses a single 2D model to learn cross-view consistency from standard B-scans and orthogonal slices, thereby generating reliable volumetric pseudo-labels. These pseudo-labels are then distilled into a 3D model, followed by a co-training stage in which the 2D and 3D models mutually refine each other through ensemble pseudo-labeling. Experiments on the MSHC and Duke DME datasets demonstrate that PC-Seg achieves accuracy comparable to fully supervised learning while using labels for only about 0.7% of the training data, outperforming state-of-the-art semi-supervised and retinal layer segmentation methods. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/gsisaoki/pc-seg-official.

</details>


### [151] [DA-Fusion: Deformable Attention-Based RGB-D Fusion Transformer for Unseen Object Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17754)
*Yesol Park,Hye-Jung Yoon,Juno Kim,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DA-Fusion是一种基于可变形注意力的RGB-D融合Transformer，用于未见物体实例分割，在杂乱物流环境中有效结合RGB和深度数据优势，并引入OCBD基准数据集，性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB方法过度分割（依赖纹理），深度方法欠分割（依赖几何特征），缺乏有效融合RGB与深度信息的方法来处理杂乱环境中的遮挡、形状变化和复杂空间排列。

Method: 提出DA-Fusion（Deformable Attention-based RGB-D Fusion Transformer），利用可变形注意力机制融合RGB和深度特征。同时构建Object Clutter Bin Dataset (OCBD)俯视评估数据集。

Result: 在多种环境下DA-Fusion均优于现有最先进方法，特别适用于物流自动化中的真实场景。

Conclusion: DA-Fusion通过可变形注意力融合RGB-D信息，有效解决了杂乱多层物体环境中的过分割和欠分割问题，为物流自动化（如抓取、拣选）提供了更可靠的感知方案。

Abstract: In logistics automation, precise segmentation of unseen objects is crucial for efficient robotic manipulation in cluttered environments. Tasks such as bin-picking and shelf-picking require robust perception to handle occlusions, varying object shapes, and complex spatial arrangements. Traditional RGB-based methods tend to over-segment objects due to their reliance on texture, while depth-based methods often under-segment by focusing primarily on geometric features. To address these limitations, we propose DA-Fusion, a deformable attention-based RGB-D fusion Transformer designed for unseen object instance segmentation. DA-Fusion effectively combines the strengths of both RGB and depth data, enhancing segmentation accuracy in cluttered and multi-layered object environments. We also introduce the Object Clutter Bin Dataset (OCBD), a benchmark dataset specifically tailored for evaluating bin-picking scenarios in top-down views. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that DA-Fusion outperforms state-of-the-art methods across diverse environments, making it particularly suited for real-world logistics tasks.

</details>


### [152] [To Blend In, First Decouple: Rethinking Camouflage Image Generation via Context-Decoupled Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17768)
*Wenzhuang Wang,Yifan Zhao,Mingcan Ma,Yunlong Che,Haoran Chen,Ming Liu,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CamoDreamer，一种上下文解耦的生成范式，通过对比感知上下文桥接、上下文解耦同化流和频率自适应上下文融合模块，解决了伪装图像生成中外观差异和背景伪影问题，在轻量级设计下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有伪装图像生成方法（背景引导或前景引导范式）存在外观不一致和背景伪影问题，根源在于跨上下文表示泄露——物体和背景线索在耦合的条件空间中纠缠，导致控制模糊和伪装保真度下降。

Method: 提出CamoDreamer，包含三个核心模块：(1) 对比感知上下文桥接，建模跨上下文差异并构建对比感知的双条件引导；(2) 上下文解耦同化流，分离基于双条件引导的生成交互，并在潜在空间中用目标感知线索辅助背景渲染；(3) 频率自适应上下文融合模块，融合解耦特征中的互补高频纹理和低频结构以提升整体连贯性。

Result: 大量实验表明CamoDreamer在显著领先现有方法的同时，保持了相对轻量级的设计。

Conclusion: 上下文解耦的生成范式能有效解决伪装图像生成中的跨上下文表示泄露问题，实现更优的伪装保真度和视觉连贯性。

Abstract: Camouflage image generation (CIG) focuses on generating visually concealed objects that seamlessly blend into their backgrounds. Existing methods typically follow either background-guided paradigms that adapt object appearance via style transfer, or foreground-guided strategies that outpaint surrounding regions conditioned on object features. However, they still suffer from appearance discrepancy and background artifacts. We attribute these limitations to cross-context representation leakage, where object and background cues are entangled in a coupled conditional space, resulting in ambiguous control and degraded camouflage fidelity. To tackle this, we propose a new context-decoupled generative paradigm, termed CamoDreamer, which aims to isolate contextual conditional guidance and explicitly decouple latent camouflage features into coordinated object and background control streams. First, a Contrast-aware Contextual Bridge is designed to model cross-context discrepancies and construct contrast-aware dual conditional guidance. Second, Context-Decoupled Assimilation Streams are employed to separate generative interactions conditioned on the dual guidance, while facilitating background rendering with target-aware cues in the latent space. Finally, a Frequency-Adaptive Contextual Blend module integrates complementary high-frequency textures and low-frequency structures from decoupled features to improve holistic coherence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CamoDreamer consistently outperforms existing methods with a substantial margin, while maintaining a relatively lightweight design.

</details>


### [153] [Measuring Monosemanticity in Sparse Autoencoders via Latent Activation Coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17770)
*Katarzyna Filus,Sebastian Pokuciński*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Tversky Monosemanticity Score (TMS)，一种无需外部标签或嵌入模型的无监督单语义性度量指标，用于评估稀疏自编码器（SAE）提取特征的可解释性。实验表明TMS对编码器各向异性不敏感，与现有单语义性指标保持一致，并能有效指示基于探针的概念删除效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单语义性度量指标要么需要外部概念标签，要么依赖预训练嵌入模型，导致其对编码器几何结构（各向异性）敏感，评估结果不稳定且缺乏通用性。

Method: 将SAE隐层激活值二值化后，基于Tversky指数计算激活集的相干性作为单语义性分数，无需外部标签或嵌入编码器。在DINOv3、CLIP、BLIP2等视觉与视觉-语言模型上，对TopK和BatchTopK两种SAE训练范式、多种稀疏度与扩展因子进行系统评估。

Result: TMS受编码器各向异性的影响显著小于基于嵌入的替代指标，同时与已有单语义性指标保持对齐；在不同基础模型上揭示了不同的SAE训练动态；在编码器各向异性条件下，TMS对基于探针的概念删除有效性提供更强的指示。

Conclusion: TMS是一种鲁棒的、标签无关的单语义性度量方法，能在不依赖外部模型的前提下有效评估SAE特征质量，特别适用于编码器存在各向异性偏差的场景。

Abstract: Within Explainable Artificial Intelligence, mechanistic interpretability uses Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to extract more interpretable features from neural representations. However, assessing their monosemanticity, and thus explanation quality, remains challenging. Existing metrics require external concept labels or depend on pretrained embedding models, making them sensitive to encoder's geometry. We introduce the Tversky Monosemanticity Score (TMS), a label-free metric that operationalizes monosemanticity as activation-set coherence of binarized SAE latents, and does not require external embedding encoders. We evaluate TMS on SAEs trained on features from pretrained vision and vision-language models (DINOv3, CLIP, BLIP2), two common SAE regimes (TopK, BatchTopK), multiple sparsity levels, and expansion factors. Our results show that TMS is less affected by encoder anisotropy than its embedding-based alternative, while remaining aligned with established monosemanticity indicators. TMS also reveals distinct SAE training dynamics across base models. Moreover, under encoder anisotropy, TMS provides a stronger indication of probe-based concept deletion effectiveness, while being competitive otherwise.

</details>


### [154] [CDIS: Cross-Dimensional Class-Agnostic 3D Instance Segmentation via 2D Mask Tracking and 3D-2D Projection Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17778)
*Juno Kim,Hye-Jung Yoon,Yesol Park,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CDIS，一种零样本的跨维度类无关3D实例分割框架，通过显式跟踪2D实例掩码并将其与3D超点关联，形成2D与3D之间的反馈循环，无需3D训练即可生成全局一致的3D实例标签，在基准测试中优于现有零样本方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类无关3D实例分割方法通常将每帧2D实例掩码投影到3D并合并，导致目标身份随时间断裂、3D实例碎片化，缺乏跨帧时间一致性和跨维度空间一致性。

Method: 提出CDIS框架，包含两个关键模块：1）跨帧2D实例掩码显式跟踪，保持时间稳定性；2）将稳定的2D轨迹与3D超点关联，构建2D到3D的反馈循环，实现跨维度推理，无需任何3D训练数据。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，CDIS在准确性和一致性上均超越现有最先进的零样本方法，且具有高效性和对多样化真实环境的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 跨维度推理能有效解决2D到3D投影中的身份断裂问题，零样本方式即可实现全局一致的3D实例分割，适用于未知环境中的机器人操作与导航任务。

Abstract: Class-agnostic 3D instance segmentation is critical for robotic systems operating in unknown environments, enabling perception of previously unseen objects for reliable manipulation and navigation. Existing approaches typically project per-frame 2D instance masks into 3D and merge them, which often breaks object identities across time and yields fragmented 3D instances. We introduce Cross-Dimensional Class-Agnostic 3D Instance Segmentation (CDIS), a zero-shot framework that explicitly tracks 2D instance masks across frames and associates them with 3D superpoints, creating a feedback loop between 2D and 3D. This cross-dimensional reasoning links temporally stable 2D tracks with spatially coherent 3D regions, producing globally consistent 3D instance labels without any 3D-specific training. Experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that CDIS achieves higher accuracy and consistency than state-of-the-art zero-shot methods, while remaining efficient and scalable to diverse real-world environments.

</details>


### [155] [BrainNext: A General-Purpose Self-Supervised Foundation Model for Brain MRI Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17782)
*Moona Mazher,Abdul Qayyum,Steven A. Niederer,Daniel C. Alexander*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BrainNext，一个基于自监督学习（掩码自编码器）和三维Bi-Directional xLSTM-UNet架构的通用脑部MRI基础模型，在60,551份未标记的多模态脑部MRI数据上预训练，在FOMO 2025挑战赛中取得第二名（脑膜瘤分割任务第一名），展示了大规模自监督预训练在脑部MRI任务中的强迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经影像基础模型受限于任务特定训练、基于切片的学习策略或较小的预训练数据集，难以在多样化的脑部MRI应用间泛化。作者旨在构建一个通用的、可扩展的自监督基础模型，以学习鲁棒且可迁移的三维体积表征。

Method: BrainNext采用掩码自编码器（MAE）预训练范式，结合原生三维Bi-Directional xLSTM-UNet架构，在60,551份涵盖多种MRI模态的未标注脑部MRI检查数据上进行自监督预学习，随后通过轻量级任务特定微调适配下游任务。

Result: 在FOMO 2025方法赛道中，BrainNext在分类、分割和脑龄估计任务上取得总体第二名，并在脑膜瘤分割任务上排名第一，验证了其在异质性神经影像任务中的强迁移能力。

Conclusion: 大规模自监督预训练能够学习鲁棒且可迁移的三维体积表征，BrainNext可作为可扩展的基础模型适用于多样化的脑部MRI应用。

Abstract: Foundation models pretrained using self-supervised learning have transformed computer vision by learning transferable representations from large-scale unlabeled data. However, existing foundation models for neuroimaging remain limited by task-specific training, slice-based learning strategies, or relatively small pretraining datasets, restricting their generalizability across diverse brain MRI applications. In this work, we present BrainNext, a general-purpose self-supervised foundation model for volumetric brain MRI analysis. BrainNext combines masked autoencoder (MAE) pretraining with a native three-dimensional Bi-Directional xLSTM-UNet architecture to learn rich anatomical representations from 60,551 unlabeled brain MRI examinations spanning multiple MRI modalities. The pretrained model is subsequently adapted to downstream tasks through lightweight task-specific fine-tuning. We evaluate BrainNext on the Foundation Models for Medical Imaging (FOMO) 2025 Method Track, encompassing classification, segmentation, and brain-age estimation, where it achieved second place overall and ranked first in the meningioma segmentation task on the official FOMO 2025 challenge leaderboard, demonstrating strong transferability across heterogeneous neuroimaging tasks. These results highlight the potential of large-scale self-supervised pretraining to learn robust and transferable volumetric representations, establishing BrainNext as a scalable foundation model for diverse brain MRI applications.

</details>


### [156] [Medical Imaging Fusing Vision Transformer: Laryngeal Cancer Screening with Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17789)
*Haiyang Wang,Luca Mainardi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Transformer和注意力机制的AI方法，结合NBI内镜图像对喉部病变进行良恶性分类（F1:82.72%, 准确率:82.33%），并利用MedSAM分割模型提供可解释性的病理区域，实现分类与分割融合的喉癌筛查方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 喉癌早期筛查对改善临床结局至关重要，但传统NBI内镜检查依赖经验丰富的临床医生，耗时长且存在观察者间差异，亟需AI辅助决策支持。

Method: 采用Transformer和注意力机制对窄带成像（NBI）内镜图像进行良恶性分类；并利用前沿分割方法MedSAM提供病理信息区域的可解释性分析，实现分类与分割的融合。

Result: 分类性能达到F1分数82.72%，准确率82.33%。

Conclusion: 提出的分类与分割融合方法为喉癌筛查提供了可行的AI辅助方案，兼具良好分类性能和临床可解释性。

Abstract: Early and timely screening of laryngeal cancer is crucial for improving clinical outcomes. In recent years, NBI endoscopy has become a standard diagnostic tool for the detection of laryngeal lesions. However, its effective use requires well-trained clinicians and the procedure is time-consuming and subject to interobserver variability. In this context, the application of artificial intelligence (AI) offers a promising solution to support clinical decision-making. In this work, we proposed applying transformer and attention mechanism for analyzing the narrow band imaging and distinguish benign and malignant lesions. Results show it has good classification performance with F1 (82.72%), accuracy(82.33%). In addition, the result of laryngeal cancer screening is explainable for clinicians. The explainability is utilizing the state of art segmentation method (MedSAM) to provide the useful pathological information area for clinicians. The proposed methodology fusing classification and segmentation provides a translating on laryngeal cancer screening.

</details>


### [157] [ReViV: Reconstructing the Viewer and the View in 4D from Monocular Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17790)
*Xiaozhong Lyu,Gen Li,Zhiyin Qian,Xucong Zhang,Marc Pollefeys,Siyu Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReViV是首个从单目RGB视频中同时重建观看者（全身运动、手部运动、凝视方向）与观看场景（相机轨迹、深度）的统一4D重建框架，基于掩码生成式自中心Transformer，实现快速且一致的4D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自中心4D重建方法依赖辅助输入（如预计算相机轨迹），将场景感知与人类自我运动建模分离，且推理速度慢。亟需一个统一、高效、无需任务特定先验的端到端框架。

Method: 将任务建模为多模态信号（RGB视频、相机轨迹、凝视、全身运动、手部运动、深度）的联合概率分布学习，提出掩码生成式自中心Transformer（Masked Generative Egocentric Transformer），采用单一前馈架构同时重建观看者与场景的时域一致4D表示。

Result: 在HoloAssist、HOT3D、ARCTIC、Aria Digital Twin、TACO五个基准上，ReViV在自我身体、手部、凝视重建和相机跟踪上达到SOTA精度，在自中心深度估计上保持高度竞争力，且无需任务特定先验。

Conclusion: ReViV证明了统一框架进行自中心4D重建的可行性与优越性，实现了速度与精度的双重提升，代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Egocentric devices, such as wearable front-facing cameras, provide a unique perspective for capturing the continuous interaction between a human viewer and the surrounding environment. A holistic and efficient multimodal model capable of reconstructing this 4D representation is therefore highly desirable. However, existing approaches often rely on auxiliary inputs such as pre-computed camera trajectories, treat scene perception and human ego-motion modeling as separate problems despite their strong interdependency, and suffer from slow inference time. To address these limitations, we present ReViV, the first unified framework for holistic egocentric 4D reconstruction that extracts both viewer and view dynamics from a single monocular RGB video. We formulate the task as learning the full joint probability distribution over multimodal signals, including RGB video, camera trajectory, gaze direction, full-body motion, hand motion, and depth. Powered by a Masked Generative Egocentric Transformer, ReViV operates within a single feed-forward architecture to simultaneously reconstruct the temporally consistent 4D reconstruction across the viewer and the view with fast inference speed. Extensive experiments on diverse benchmarks, including HoloAssist, HOT3D, ARCTIC, Aria Digital Twin, and TACO, demonstrate that ReViV achieves state-of-the-art accuracy and efficiency across holistic ego-body, hand, and gaze reconstruction, camera tracking, while maintaining highly competitive egocentric depth estimation without relying on heavy task-specific priors. Code and models are fully open-sourced: https://reviv4d.github.io/.

</details>


### [158] [Toward Optimal Adenovirus Detection Using YOLO26](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17799)
*Olivier Rukundo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究系统比较了四种数据增强方法（NAS、GAS、GMAS、DAS）在YOLO26模型不同规模变体上对透射电镜图像中腺病毒检测效果的影响，通过重新标注公开TEM病毒数据集中的腺病毒颗粒位置生成YOLO兼容的边界框注释，并在相同训练条件下评估各增强策略的性能，最终确定了最有效的数据增强方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在透射电子显微镜（TEM）图像中准确检测腺病毒颗粒对病毒研究和诊断至关重要，但数据增强策略的选择缺乏系统性比较，现有研究未针对特定病毒检测任务进行数据增强方案的优化，因此需要系统评估不同数据增强设置在YOLO模型上的表现，以确定最有效的方案。

Method: 选取公开TEM病毒数据集中的腺病毒子集，利用腺病毒颗粒位置信息重新标注生成YOLO兼容的边界框标注。在相同训练条件下，对YOLO26模型的不同规模变体分别应用四种数据增强设置：NAS、GAS、GMAS和DAS，进行系统性基准测试。

Result: 实验结果表明，不同的数据增强设置对YOLO26模型检测腺病毒的性能有显著影响，且在不同模型规模变体上的表现存在差异，最终通过对比确定了最有效的数据增强方案。

Conclusion: 系统性的数据增强基准测试有助于优化TEM图像中腺病毒的自动检测性能，为后续病毒颗粒检测任务中的数据增强选择提供了参考依据。

Abstract: This study systematically benchmarks different data augmentation setups across YOLO26 model size variants to determine the most effective setup for adenovirus detection in TEM images. The benchmarked setups include NAS, GAS, GMAS and DAS, all evaluated under identical training conditions. The adenovirus dataset, selected from the published TEM virus dataset, was re-annotated by leveraging adenovirus particle positions to generate YOLO-compatible bounding box annotations. The experimental results demonstrated the impact of the benchmarked data augmentation setups on adenovirus detection with YOLO26 and indicated the most effective data augmentation setup.

</details>


### [159] [FF-ProCams: Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting for Projector-Camera System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17803)
*Ziyao Wang,Yuqi Li,Wenxing Zheng,Jiaying Chen,Chong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FF-ProCams提出了一种前馈式3D高斯逆渲染框架，能在单次前向传播中从稀疏多视角观测预测可重照明的3D高斯表示，实现投影仪-相机系统的高保真、可泛化渲染，仅需8张输入视图即可在0.13秒内完成重建，相比优化基线方法速度提升3-5个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ProCams逆渲染方法存在精度-效率权衡：基于优化的方法精度高但需耗时的逐场景优化；前馈3D重建模型虽快但无法适应投影仪的空间变化照明。

Method: 构建混合Mamba2-Transformer编码器聚合跨视角几何与光度线索，通过轻量级预测头在单次前向传播中输出可重照明3D高斯表示；设计投影仪感知的可微渲染器，支持任意主动照明与ProCams位姿下的相机观测合成；构建大规模合成ProCams数据集用于前馈训练。

Result: 仅需8张输入视图即可实现高保真实时渲染，超越使用297视图的优化基线方法，测试时重建仅需0.13秒（速度提升3-5个数量级），且能泛化到未见过的投影图案和新的投影仪-相机位姿。

Conclusion: FF-ProCams有效解决了ProCams逆渲染中精度与效率的权衡，为空间增强现实、投影映射和表面反射率获取等应用提供了快速且高质量的解决方案。

Abstract: Projector-camera (ProCams) systems achieve active scene perception and controllable appearance manipulation via structured illumination, serving as a core infrastructure for spatial augmented reality, projection mapping, and surface reflectance acquisition. Existing inverse-rendering methods for ProCams deliver high-fidelity results but rely on time-consuming per-scene optimization, while mainstream feed-forward 3D reconstruction models produce baked appearance that cannot adapt to spatially varying projector illumination.
  To resolve this accuracy-efficiency trade-off, we propose FF-ProCams, a Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian inverse-rendering framework for ProCams. A hybrid Mamba2-Transformer encoder aggregates cross-view geometric and photometric cues from sparse multi-view observations, and lightweight heads predict a relightable Gaussian representation in a single forward pass. We further design a projector-aware differentiable renderer to synthesize camera observations under arbitrary active illumination and ProCams poses. To enable feed-forward training, we construct a large-scale synthetic ProCams dataset covering diverse object geometries and surface materials. Experiments show FF-ProCams achieves high-fidelity projector-aware rendering, generalizes to unseen patterns, and supports novel projector-camera poses. Using only 8 input views, it outperforms optimization-based baselines with 297 views while reducing test-time reconstruction to 0.13 seconds (a three-to-five-order-of-magnitude speedup). The code and data are available at https://github.com/CPREgroup/FF-ProCams/.

</details>


### [160] [Vis2Reg: Visibility-Aware Landmark-Free Geometric 3D--2D Registration for Liver Laparoscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17810)
*Jiaming Feng,Xukun Zhang,Shahid Farid,Sharib Ali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Vis2Reg提出了一种基于可见性感知的3D-2D肝脏配准框架，通过掩膜引导的可见域自监督信号和可微点光栅化技术，实现了从术前3D模型到术中部分观测表面的鲁棒对齐，在真实术中数据集上达到92.6% Dice分数和1.43mm Chamfer距离，推理速度111ms/帧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AR引导腹腔镜手术需要精确的3D-2D肝脏配准，但现有方法面临严重遮挡、有限视野和缺乏3D监督信号的挑战，尤其极端部分可见性下的鲁棒自监督学习难以实现。

Method: 提出Vis2Reg框架：1）可见性感知自监督机制，通过可微点光栅化和掩膜引导反投影从术中掩膜推导可见域3D监督信号；2）鲁棒几何刚性初始化模块；3）隐式神经变形场实现稳定对齐。

Result: 在真实术中数据集上Dice分数92.6%，Chamfer距离1.43mm，单帧推理时间111ms。

Conclusion: Vis2Reg通过可见性感知的掩膜约束和自监督学习有效解决了极端部分可见性下的鲁棒3D-2D配准问题，兼具精度与实时性，适用于AR引导腹腔镜手术。

Abstract: Accurate 3D--2D liver registration, which aligns preoperative 3D models to partial, view-dependent intraoperative surface observations, is critical for AR-guided laparoscopic surgery but remains challenging due to severe occlusion, limited visibility, and the lack of 3D ground-truth supervision. Existing landmark-free approaches perform partial-to-complete geometric alignment, yet robust self-supervision under extreme partial visibility remains difficult. We propose Vis2Reg, a visibility-aware registration framework that explicitly constrains deformation using mask-consistent visible regions. We introduce a visibility-aware self-supervision that derives a visible-domain 3D supervision signal from intraoperative masks, enabled by differentiable point rasterization and mask-guided back-projection. This formulation improves robustness under severe occlusion while maintaining fully self-supervised learning. Vis2Reg combines a robust geometric rigid initialization module with an implicit neural deformation field for stable alignment. Vis2Reg achieves a Dice score of 92.6\% and a Chamfer Distance of 1.43 mm on real intraoperative datasets, with 111 ms per-frame inference time, demonstrating both accuracy and practical efficiency.

</details>


### [161] [PRiSM: Prototype Regularization for Few-Shot VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17820)
*Ghassen Baklouti,Omprakash Chakraborty,Jose Dolz,Ismail Ben Ayed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的少样本适应基准测试，通过Dirichlet采样改变类别平衡和有效类别数量，发现现有方法性能显著下降。为此提出PRiSM，一种即插即用的类原型正则化方法，通过多目标损失函数和块Majorize-Minimize优化器，有效提升了训练-free方法在类别不平衡和高类别数场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练-free少样本适应方法的基准测试对适应数据统计特性（如类别平衡）存在强假设，这些假设过于简化且不现实。需要引入更真实的基准测试来评估方法在类别不平衡和不同有效类别数下的表现。

Method: 提出PRiSM，包含三部分：(1) 多目标损失函数，包括最大化类间成对距离的正则化项、支持特征对齐项和基线原型保真项；(2) 通过Gershgorin圆定理计算有效的块状Lipschitz常数（Hessian谱范数边界）；(3) 基于块Majorize-Minimize的高效优化器。

Result: 在Dirichlet采样的新基准下，现有SOTA方法性能显著下降，且标记样本增多时下降更严重。PRiSM作为即插即用模块显著提升了多种训练-free基线方法的性能，尤其在严重类别不平衡和高类别数场景下增益最大。

Conclusion: 真实场景中的类别不平衡问题对现有少样本适应方法构成重大挑战，PRiSM通过类原型正则化有效缓解了这一问题，且具有即插即用的实用性和计算高效性。

Abstract: Training-free few-shot adaptation methods have gained significant attention recently in the context of Vision-language Models (VLMs). Yet, current benchmarks rely on strong assumptions about the statistics of the adaptation data, e.g., class balance. We question these simplifying assumptions and introduce a more realistic benchmark that varies both the levels of class balance and the effective number of classes in few-shot tasks via Dirichlet sampling. Surprisingly, under our setting, we observe substantial drops in the performances of state-of-the-art methods, more so when the number of labeled samples increases. To mitigate this, we introduce PRiSM, a class-prototype regularization that can be deployed as a plug and play module on top of any existing baseline method, significantly improving performances. Our method optimizes a novel multi-term loss, which includes a regularizer maximizing inter-class pairwise distances, along with additional terms promoting support-feature alignment and fidelity to the baseline prototypes. Furthermore, we introduce an effective and computationally efficient block Majorize-Minimize optimizer for our objective. More specifically, we derive a valid blockwise Lipschitz constant (i.e., a bound on the Hessian's spectral norm), which can be computed efficiently via the Gershgorin circle theorem. Extensive experiments show that PRiSM improves several training-free baselines, with large gains when dealing with severe class imbalance and high numbers of classes.

</details>


### [162] [Consistent Feature Transport for Image Relighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17833)
*Bohan Zhang,Huanwei Liang,Yuhan He,Hongteng Xu,Quxiao Chao,Luoqi Liu,Dixin Luo,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一致特征传输（CFT）框架，将图像重照明重新定义为光照特征传输问题，基于整流流（rectified flow）联合建模噪声到图像生成与光照一致的源到目标传输，通过轨迹级监督分离光照变化并保留内容特征，在大型人像重照明数据集上优于现有方法，并可推广到风格迁移等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的重照明方法缺乏显式学习图像间特征变换的机制，在复杂光照下常出现光照变化不稳定或内容保持不一致的问题。

Method: 将重照明重新表述为光照特征传输问题，提出CFT训练原则，基于整流流（rectified flow）联合建模噪声到图像生成和光照一致的源到目标传输，通过轨迹级监督实现双传输过程，分离光照变化并保留内容特征。

Result: 在构建的大型人像重照明数据集上，CFT在各项指标上一致优于现有最先进方法，并能泛化到风格迁移等其他编辑任务。

Conclusion: CFT通过显式的特征传输机制有效解决了复杂光照下的重照明问题，为图像编辑提供了一种统一框架。

Abstract: Image relighting modifies illumination while preserving non-lighting content such as identity and geometry. Existing diffusion-based methods often suffer from unstable illumination changes or inconsistent content preservation under complex lighting, as they lack an explicit mechanism to learn feature transformations between images. We reformulate relighting as an illumination feature transport problem and introduce Consistent Feature Transport (CFT), a training principle that explicitly enforces illumination-consistent transport between source and target image distributions. Built upon rectified flow, CFT jointly models noise-to-image generation and illumination-consistent source-to-target transport through trajectory-level supervision. This dual-transport formulation encourages isolation of illumination-specific variations while preserving content-aligned features. To support complex lighting scenarios, we construct a large-scale portrait relighting dataset with diverse relighting effects. Experiments show consistent improvements over existing state-of-the-art relighting approaches and demonstrate that CFT can generalize to other editing tasks, including style transfer. Code is available at https://github.com/Dixin-Lab/CFT.

</details>


### [163] [Measuring and Improving Complex-Atomic Answer Consistency in Endoscopic VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17834)
*Yuhao Liu,Cheng Zhao,Guanghui Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EndoCA基准，用于评估内窥镜VQA中复杂答案与原子答案的一致性，并引入ASR机制（无需训练）通过原子答案作为上下文前提来修正答案或选择性回答，从而减少复杂-原子不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内窥镜VQA中复杂问题通常包含多个答案组件，现有评估只关注复杂答案正确性，忽略了模型在构成该复杂答案的原子问题上的表现，导致即使模型在原子问题上失败，复杂答案仍可能被判定为正确，缺乏对答案一致性的考量。

Method: 构建EndoCA基准（包含Core和Diagnostic两个子集），评估11种VLM在复杂-原子答案一致性上的表现；提出ASR机制，利用模型自生成的原子答案作为上下文前提，通过ASR-Revise（答案修正）和ASR-Selective（选择性回答）两种策略在无需训练的情况下提升一致性。

Result: 部分VLM在复杂答案准确率上表现良好，但原子答案准确率和复杂-原子一致性显著偏低；ASR-Revise在少量改变复杂答案准确率的同时提升配对复杂-原子正确性，ASR-Selective通过让模型放弃低可靠性样本提升了已回答样本的准确率。

Conclusion: EndoCA和ASR分别提供了一致性感知的基准测试和无需训练的答案调和与选择性回答机制，为内窥镜VQA中的一致性评估与改进提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Endoscopic visual question answering (VQA) increasingly asks complex questions that combine several endoscopic answer components rather than isolated factual queries. Such complex answers may be scored as correct even when the same model fails on associated atomic questions. We introduce EndoCA, a paired complex-atomic answer consistency benchmark for evaluating whether complex answers remain consistent with same-image atomic answers. EndoCA contains two suites: EndoCA-Core evaluates compact question-complexity patterns commonly seen in practical endoscopic VQA, and EndoCA-Diagnostic supports controlled analysis across increasing question complexity. We evaluate 11 VLMs spanning open, medical, endoscopy-adapted, and closed-source models on EndoCA. Some VLMs achieve high complex-answer accuracy, yet their atomic-answer accuracy and complex-atomic answer consistency remain substantially lower. To reduce this complex-atomic inconsistency, we introduce Atomic-Support Reconciliation (ASR), a training-free mechanism that uses model-generated atomic answers as contextual premises for answer revision and consistency-guided selective answering. On four selected publicly available models, ASR-Revise improves paired complex-atomic correctness with modest changes in complex-answer accuracy, while ASR-Selective improves accuracy on answered cases by allowing the model to abstain from less reliable cases. Together, EndoCA and ASR provide a consistency-aware benchmark and a training-free mechanism for answer reconciliation and selective answering in endoscopic VQA.

</details>


### [164] [CaT-GS: Efficient 3DGS Rendering for Large Scale Scenes via Inter-frame Caching and Tile Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17842)
*Tingjia Zhang,Bo Chen,Shengzhong Liu,Fan Wu,Guihai Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CaT-GS提出了一种高效的3D高斯泼溅渲染流水线，通过推测性多帧预处理、帧间缓存机制和专用内核重构光栅化任务，在大型场景中实现高达10倍于原始3DGS的加速，并较之前最先进方法提升70%速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS在大型场景中性能显著下降，现有优化方法要么需要昂贵的重新训练，要么只关注流水线的狭窄方面，忽略了实际部署中的关键低效问题。

Method: 1) 推测性多帧预处理方法，消除连续帧间的冗余计算；2) 帧间缓存机制，消除视点冗余渲染阶段；3) 专用内核重构光栅化任务，缓解图块负载不均衡，提升GPU利用率。

Result: CaT-GS在大型场景中实现高达10倍于原始3DGS的加速，并较之前最先进方法提升70%速度。

Conclusion: CaT-GS通过系统性识别并解决3DGS渲染流水线中的三项关键冗余和低效问题，建立了高保真实时大型场景渲染的新基准。

Abstract: Recent breakthroughs in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have advanced neural rendering with high fidelity and speed. However, its performance degrades significantly in large-scale scenes due to the computational burden of tile-based rasterization. Existing optimization efforts either require costly scene re-training or focus on narrow aspects of the pipeline, overlooking critical inefficiencies in real-world deployments. Through a comprehensive analysis, we identify three primary sources of redundancy and low GPU utilization: redundant inter-frame pre-processing, viewpoint-based occlusion redundancy, and severe tile-level load imbalance. To address these issues, we propose CaT-GS, a novel and efficient 3DGS rendering pipeline. CaT-GS introduces a speculative multi-frame preprocessing method to eliminate redundant computations across consecutive frames, and an inter-frame caching mechanism to eliminate viewpoint redundant rendering stages. Furthermore, it refactors rasterization tasks with a dedicated kernel to mitigate tile load imbalance, significantly boosting GPU utilization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CaT-GS achieves a speedup of up to 10 times over the original 3DGS and up to 70% over previous state-of-the-art methods, establishing a new benchmark for high-fidelity, real-time rendering of large-scale scenes.

</details>


### [165] [Leveraging Dissimilarity Invariance as a Robust Anchor for Learning with Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17857)
*Wenxiao Fan,Kan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现标签噪声下语义不相似性保持稳定（不相似性不变性），并提出NegScale框架，通过结构负正交惩罚和不相似性校准相似度调整，在合成与真实噪声数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度模型在标签噪声下性能严重下降，现有方法因无法学习准确相似性而误导训练过程，亟需更鲁棒的学习范式。

Method: 提出NegScale框架，包含：(1) 结构负正交惩罚项(SNOP)，强制不相关样本的子空间正交；(2) 不相似性校准的相似度调整(DCSA)，利用不相似性锚点抑制虚假相似性；并给出不相似性不变性的理论证明。

Result: NegScale在CIFAR合成噪声和真实噪声数据集上一致超越现有SOTA基线，建立新基准。

Conclusion: 不相似性不变性是对标签噪声鲁棒的有效特性，NegScale通过将关注点从脆弱的相似性转向鲁棒的不相似性，显著提升了抗噪学习性能。

Abstract: Deep learning models excel in visual recognition but suffer severe performance drops when training labels are corrupted by noise. Under label noise prior work cannot learn accurate similarities and thus misguide the learning process. In this paper, we uncover a complementary and novel phenomenon, Dissimilarity Invariance, whereby semantic dissimilarity between unrelated samples remains stable despite label noise. Leveraging this insight, we propose NegScale, a plug-and-play framework that shifts focus from fragile similarity to robust dissimilarity. NegScale integrates: (1) Structured Negative Orthogonality Penalty (SNOP), enforcing subspace orthogonality for unrelated samples; and (2) Dissimilarity-Calibrated Similarity Adjustment (DCSA), suppressing spurious similarity using dissimilarity anchors. We also give theoretical analysis that proves Dissimilarity Invariance and the effectiveness of NegScale. Empirical results demonstrate that NegScale consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, establishing new benchmarks on CIFAR with synthetic noise and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [166] [Locality-Aware Density Control for Efficient Gaussian-based Image Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17896)
*Jiacong Chen,Qingyu Mao,Xiandong Meng,Shuai Liu,Chao Li,Fanyang Meng,Youneng Bao,Yongsheng Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Locality-Aware Density Control (LocoADC)框架，通过区域级高斯致密化（RGD）和相似性驱动高斯合并（SDGM）两种策略，联合优化2D高斯泼溅表示中的高斯容量分配，解决欠重建区域的容量不足和过重建区域的冗余问题。在多个数据集上，LocoADC在相同高斯预算下显著提升基线性能（如在CLIC数据集上获得2.93 dB PSNR提升）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有2D高斯泼溅方法在优化过程中未能高效分配高斯容量：欠重建区域以碎片化像素级方式精化，而过重建区域中相邻的已优化高斯因属性相似被冗余保留。这种低效性促使需要一个密度控制框架，联合处理欠重建区域的容量不足和过重建区域的冗余分配问题。

Method: 提出Locality-Aware Density Control (LocoADC)框架，利用两种互补的局部性：图像空间中重建误差的局部连续性以改善高斯分配，以及高斯空间中相邻高斯的局部相似性以消除冗余。核心组件包括：1) Region-wise Gaussian Densification (RGD)——区域级高斯致密化策略；2) Similarity-Driven Gaussian Merging (SDGM)——相似性驱动高斯合并策略；3) 局部颜色一致性约束，用于更可靠的合并。

Result: 在多个数据集上的广泛实验表明，LocoADC通过启用更有效的局部高斯分配，持续改进多个基线方法。在CLIC数据集上，相同30k高斯预算下，相较GI方法获得2.93 dB的PSNR增益。

Conclusion: LocoADC作为即插即用框架，有效提升了2D高斯泼溅表示中高斯容量的利用率，通过区域级致密化和相似性合并实现了更优的容量分配。

Abstract: 2D Gaussian Splatting is an attractive direction for image representation due to its explicit formulation, fast rasterization, and favorable decoding efficiency. The representation quality of this paradigm depends on the proper allocation of Gaussian capacity to the demanding regions. However, existing methods fail to allocate Gaussian capacity efficiently during optimization: under-reconstructed content is often refined in a fragmented pixel-wise manner, while neighboring optimized Gaussians with similar attributes are redundantly retained. This inefficiency motivates the need for a density control framework that jointly addresses insufficient allocation in under-reconstructed regions and redundant allocation in over-reconstructed regions. Our key insight is that this framework should exploit two complementary forms of locality: the local continuity of reconstruction errors in image space for improved Gaussian allocation, and the local similarity of neighboring Gaussians in Gaussian space for redundant elimination. Based on this insight, we propose Locality-Aware Density Control (LocoADC), a plug-and-play framework that improves Gaussian capacity utilization through Region-wise Gaussian Densification (RGD) and Similarity-Driven Gaussian Merging (SDGM) strategies, together with a local color consistency constraint for more reliable merging. Extensive experiments on diverse datasets show that LocoADC consistently improves multiple baselines by enabling more effective local Gaussian allocation, including a 2.93 dB PSNR gain over GI on the CLIC dataset under the same 30k Gaussian budget. Code is available at: \textit{https://github.com/ChenJiaCong-1005/LocoADC}.

</details>


### [167] [MuViSeg: Multi-View Segment Correspondences from Dense Geometry Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17938)
*Denis Fatykhoph,Timur Akhtyamov,Konstantin Pakulev,German Devchich,Gonzalo Ferrer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出三种学习型匹配头（LightGlue风格注意力头、DPT风格多尺度融合模块、多视图联合自注意力头），用于提升类无关实例分割级别的图像匹配性能。其中多视图扩展是主要贡献，能恢复严格两两匹配无法获得的传递对应关系。在Replica和Virtual KITTI 2数据集上LightGlue头相比无参数Sinkhorn匹配器提升显著；在HM3D实例图像导航基准上，多视图变体将成功率从50%提升至70%，LightGlue头将SPL从45.7提升至59.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像匹配方法在稀疏关键点或密集像素级别进行，但下游任务（如目标级映射、拓扑导航、场景图维护）是在物体整体层面推理的。近期工作通过实例分割级别的直接匹配来缩小这一差距，但现有方法仍缺乏学习型匹配头和多视图联合推理能力，导致传递对应关系缺失。

Method: 1) LightGlue风格的注意力头（在冻结的MASt3R描述子上使用DoubleSoftmax评分）；2) DPT风格的多尺度融合模块（在VGGT基础模型池化前暴露分层空间细节）；3) 多视图扩展（主要贡献）：对多个视图的片段进行联合自注意力，恢复传递对应关系。

Result: 在Replica和Virtual KITTI 2上的零样本评估中，LightGlue头相比无参数Sinkhorn匹配器在MASt3R骨干上分别提升+4.85和+25.9 AUPRC。在HM3D实例图像导航基准上，多视图变体将成功率从50%提升至70%，LightGlue头将SPL从45.7提升至59.1。

Conclusion: 学习型匹配头（尤其是多视图联合自注意力机制）能显著提升实例级图像匹配性能及下游拓扑导航任务的成功率和路径效率，证明了在分段级匹配范式中引入学习型组件和多视图推理的有效性。

Abstract: Classical image correspondence is solved at the level of sparse keypoints or dense pixels, but the systems that consume these matches - object-level mapping, topological navigation, scene-graph maintenance - reason about whole objects. Recent work narrows this gap by matchng directly at the level of instance segments: a class-agnostic segmenter partitions each image, and per-segment descriptors are obtained by pooling features from large 3D foundation models over the masks. We build on this segment-level matching paradigm and propose three learned matching heads: a LightGlue-style attention head with DoubleSoftmax scoring on frozen MASt3R descriptors; a DPT-style multi-scale fusion module that exposes layered spatial detail from the VGGT foundation model before pooling; and - as our main contribution - a multi-view extension that performs joint self-attention over segments drawn from several views at once, recovering transitive correspondences that strictly pairwise matchers cannot reach. Under a stratified zero-shot protocol on Replica and Virtual KITTI 2 with controlled viewpoint baselines from 0 deg to 180 deg, the LightGlue-style head improves over a parameter-free Sinkhorn matcher on the same MASt3R backbone by +4.85 AUPRC on Replica and +25.9 AUPRC on Virtual KITTI 2. Dropped into the RoboHop topological navigation pipeline on the Habitat-Matterport 3D (HM3D) Instance Image Navigation benchmark without retraining, our multi-view variant raises success rate from 50% to 70%, and our LightGlue-style head raises SPL from 45.7 to 59.1.

</details>


### [168] [Exploration Matters for Escaping the Blur Trap in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17965)
*Chengbo Wang,Guozheng Ma,Jinhong Wu,Tie Ji,Yizhen Lao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)优化中存在由梯度偏差导致的“模糊陷阱”(Blur Trap)问题，将其分为远侧和近侧两类，并提出了两种简单的探索策略（随机播种和随机分割）来分别缓解这两类陷阱，从而提升渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS在优化过程中因显式建模引入的梯度偏差，导致非凸优化容易收敛到局部次优解（即Blur Trap），限制了重建质量。

Method: 首先从数学上分析3DGS优化公式，识别出导致Blur Trap的优化偏差并将其分为远侧和近侧两类；然后分别提出随机播种(Random Seeding)和随机分割(Random Splitting)两种探索策略来缓解两类陷阱。

Result: 实验表明，加入这两种简单的探索算子能够有效且互补地克服Blur Trap，在多个数据集上实现高质量的渲染效果。

Conclusion: 本文首次识别并命名了3DGS优化中的Blur Trap问题，并通过简单的显式探索策略有效缓解了该问题，显著提升了渲染性能。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) employs Gaussian primitives for explicit scene representation, facilitating real-time, high-fidelity reconstruction and novel view synthesis of complex scenes. However, the explicit modeling inherent in 3DGS introduces a gradient bias during optimization, rendering its non-convex optimization process highly susceptible to convergence toward local suboptimal solutions. This constitutes a fundamental limitation in 3DGS optimization, which we term the Blur Trap. To address this limitation, we integrate simple explicit exploration into the 3DGS optimization framework. First, through rigorous mathematical analysis of the 3DGS optimization formulation, we identify the underlying optimization bias responsible for the Blur Trap and categorize it into two distinct subtypes: the Far-Side Blur Trap and the Near-Side Blur Trap. Subsequently, we propose two highly straightforward exploration strategies (Random Seeding and Random Splitting) to mitigate the far-side and near-side blur traps, respectively. Experimental validation demonstrates that the incorporation of these exploration operators effectively and complementarily overcome the Blur Trap, achieving high-quality rendering performance across multiple datasets. Project page: https://chengbo-wang.github.io/ExploreGS/

</details>


### [169] [Fine-Detail Monocular Geometry Estimation with Self-Guided Sparse Volumetric Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17967)
*Lingyu Kong,Ruicheng Li,Ruicheng Wang,Sicheng Xu,Chengtang Yao,Jianfeng Xiang,Jiaolong Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自引导稀疏3D细化（SSR）的单目几何估计方法，将粗粒度点云从图像空间提升到稀疏体素壳中进行3D空间细化，通过稀疏卷积基于3D空间局部性聚合特征，避免深度不连续处的特征混合，从而显著提升薄结构和小物体等细节的3D几何重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目几何估计模型在2D参数化中解码3D几何，特征交互受图像平面邻近性而非真实3D空间关系主导，导致薄结构、小物体等精细细节处出现过度平滑和局部3D结构失真。

Method: 提出SSR框架：1) 从基础模型（如单目深度估计基础模型）获取粗粒度点云；2) 将粗点云提升到稀疏体素壳中；3) 在稀疏体素壳上通过稀疏卷积进行3D空间细化，基于3D空间局部性聚合特征，避免深度间断处的特征混合。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验显示，该方法在定量指标和可视化效果上均显著优于现有方法，特别是在恢复精细3D几何细节方面。

Conclusion: 将单目几何建模从2D图像空间提升到3D空间进行细化，利用稀疏卷积的3D空间局部性特征聚合，能有效解决现有方法在精细结构上的几何失真问题，实现高保真的度量尺度点图估计。

Abstract: Monocular geometry estimation has recently achieved impressive performance across diverse scenes. However, state-of-the-art models still face notable distortion in local 3D structure, especially in fine details, like thin structures and small objects. We attribute this limitation to an architectural mismatch: most current models decode 3D geometry within a 2D parameterization, where feature interactions are governed by image-plane proximity rather than true 3D spatial relationships. This inadvertently mixes features from geometrically distant surfaces, resulting in over-smoothed geometry particularly around thin or elongated structure. In this paper, we propose a fine-detail monocular geometry estimation with Self-Guided Sparse 3D Refinement (SSR) that lifts monocular geometry modeling from 2D image space to 3D space for high-fidelity metric-scale point maps. Our model lifts the coarse point map from a foundation base model onto a sparse voxel shell and refines it via SSR. The SSR employs sparse convolutions that aggregate features based on 3D spatial locality, avoiding feature mixing across depth discontinuities. Extensive experiments on diverse datasets demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing approaches in recovering fine detailed 3D geometry across both quantitative metrics and qualitative visualizations.

</details>


### [170] [HAS: Highlight-guided Attention Steering for Multimodal LLM Video Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17994)
*Rui Chu,Yingjie Lao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HAS是一种基于高亮引导注意力转向的视频摘要方法，通过全局连续帧级高亮分布指导MLLM在推理时聚焦高亮帧同时不丢失低高亮帧信息，显著提升视频摘要性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频摘要方法仅依赖离散选择的关键帧及其片段描述，忽视了帧的全局重要性视角，导致理解连贯性降低、重要信息丢失，且未能充分利用MLLM的原始能力。

Method: HAS包含两部分：1）全局连续帧级高亮分布提取；2）将该高亮分布作为注意力转向向量注入MLLM，使模型在高亮帧上分配更多注意力，同时在低高亮帧上减少注意力而非直接遗忘。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上评估，HAS在视频摘要任务上展现了令人信服的性能。

Conclusion: 通过全局高亮引导的连续帧注意力转向，能够更有效地利用MLLM进行视频摘要，既保持理解连贯性又避免信息丢失。

Abstract: Video understanding has become more and more important with the growth of Artificial Intelligence (AI) for video generation. Recently, Multimodal Large Language Model(M-LLM) has shown its capability in video understanding. Video summarization, a specific domain of video understanding, has proven its importance for efficient navigation and retrieval. Both video understanding and video summarization require a good selection of key frames in a video. Current video summarization methods heavily focus on the selected key frames and correlated segment captions. However, existing approaches overlook the perspective of treating the importance of the frames globally. We argue that using discrete selected frames for summarization will not only reduce the understanding coherence, but also lost important information in the video, as well as wasting the original capacity of the MLLMs. In this paper, we propose HAS, a Highlight-guided Attention Steering method for video summarization. We consider a challenging but practical setting where the video given to MLLMs for summarize should be continuous but with highlight guidance. HAS mainly consists of two parts: The first part is to find a continuous frame-level highlight distribution for the video globally. The second part is to apply the highlight distribution as an attention steering vector for the MLLM, targeting a better understanding of the video, and thus during the model inference time, putting more attention on the highlighted frames, while avoiding lost entire information on less highlighted frames through putting less attention instead of forgetting them. We evaluated HAS on a variety of benchmarks, and it has shown convincing performance in video summarization.

</details>


### [171] [SAMRI-3D: Adapting SAM2 for 3D MRI Segmentation with Global Volume Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18014)
*Zhao Wang,Wei Dai,Hongfu Sun,Craig Engstrom,Shekhar S. Chandra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAMRI-3D 是一个面向 3D MRI 分割的 benchmark 和方法，基于 SAM2 构建，在 34 个数据集、10,392 个 volume 上评估。通过冻结图像编码器、微调解码器和记忆模块，将 Dice 从 0.58 提升至 0.76；进一步引入全局体积令牌（GVT）和截断符号距离场（TSDF）重建目标，实现 0.78 的平均 Dice，并在 8 个未见数据集上无性能下降（0.79 vs 0.78），超越现有 SAM 医疗模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SAM 模型对医学影像的适配多为通用模型，缺乏针对 MRI 的大规模建模和基准测试；MRI 软组织对比度低，许多边界在单切片上不可见，亟需专门的方法和评估标准。

Method: 基于 SAM2，冻结图像编码器，仅微调解码器和记忆模块；提出全局体积令牌（GVT），以截断符号距离场（TSDF）重建为训练目标，推理时丢弃（零额外成本）。

Result: 在 34 个数据集（27 公开 + 7 内部）、10,392 个体积上，微调模型 Dice 0.76，完整 SAMRI-3D 达 0.78，超越 SAMed-2（0.69）、Medical-SAM2（0.49）、SAM-Med3D（0.37）；8 个未见数据集上未见性能下降（0.79 vs 0.78），TSDF 目标在低对比度切片上帮助最大。

Conclusion: SAMRI-3D 是目前最大规模的 MRI 分割基准和方法，通过轻量微调和 GVT 设计，显著提升了 SAM2 在 MRI 上的分割精度，尤其适用于低对比度边界，具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Foundation models such as Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2) have transformed natural-image and video segmentation, and recent work has begun adapting them to medical imaging. These adaptations, however, are largely general-purpose models that treat MRI as one modality among many; large-scale, MRI-specific modelling and benchmarking remain limited, even though MRI's low soft-tissue contrast leaves many boundaries effectively invisible on individual slices. We present SAMRI-3D, a benchmark and method for 3D MRI segmentation with SAM2. The SAMRI-3D benchmark is the largest MRI-only evaluation to date - 10,392 volumes from 34 datasets (27 public, 7 in-house) spanning 12 anatomical domains and 10+ sequences, with explicit seen/unseen splits. Freezing the image encoder and fine-tuning only the lightweight decoder and memory modules raises mean Dice from 0.58 (zero-shot SAM2) to 0.76, surpassing recent SAM-based medical models (SAMed-2 0.69, Medical-SAM2 0.49, SAM-Med3D 0.37) with strong statistical significance. To target invisible boundaries, we introduce Global Volume Tokens (GVT): persistent memory tokens trained with a Truncated Signed Distance Field (TSDF) reconstruction objective that is discarded at inference (zero added cost). This full model, SAMRI-3D, attains the best accuracy (0.78) and lowest variance across all 34 datasets and, uniquely, shows no drop on 8 held-out datasets (0.79 unseen vs. 0.78 seen); per-sequence analysis confirms the TSDF objective helps most where per-slice contrast is weakest. We will release the benchmark, code, and models in this paper.

</details>


### [172] [Benchmarking NACTI Species Recognition in Long-Tailed Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18033)
*Zehua Liu,Tilo Burghardt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多种长尾识别（LTR）方法在北美相机陷阱图像（NACTI）数据集上的物种识别性能，优化配置达到了99.40%的Top-1准确率。但在严重域偏移下，LTR方法仍无法克服尾部类别的表征瓶颈，导致预测崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NACTI数据集存在严重的长尾类别不平衡问题（最大类别占370万图像的50%以上），标准模型在尾部类别和域偏移场景下泛化能力不足，亟需系统评估LTR方法的有效性。

Method: 基于PyTorch Wildlife模型，系统评估了多种LTR方法（包括专用损失函数和LTR敏感正则化），并在三个独立降偏测试集（ENA-Detection、Caltech Camera Traps、Missouri Camera Traps）上进行域偏移鲁棒性评估。

Result: 优化配置在NACTI测试集上达到99.40% Top-1准确率；在OOD评估中，LTR增强模型相比标准交叉熵方法表现出更强的泛化能力；但严重域偏移下尾部类别预测出现灾难性崩溃。

Conclusion: 当前LTR优化无法完全克服表征瓶颈，尤其在严重域偏移下对稀有尾部类别的预测仍会崩溃，表明需要进一步探索更鲁棒的长尾识别方法。

Abstract: As with most ``in the wild'' collections of the natural world, the North America Camera Trap Images (NACTI) dataset exhibits long-tailed class imbalance, with the largest class covering over 50% of its 3.7M images. Building on the PyTorch Wildlife model, we systematically evaluate Long-Tail Recognition (LTR) methodologies to benchmark species recognition performance, including specialised loss functions and LTR-sensitive regularisation. Our optimised configuration achieves state-of-the-art 99.40% Top-1 accuracy on the NACTI test split, significantly outperforming standard baselines and previously reported top performances. To assess robustness under domain shifts (e.g., night-time captures, occlusion, motion-blur), we extend our evaluation across three independent reduced-bias test sets (including ENA-Detection, Caltech Camera Traps and Missouri Camera Traps). Across these out-of-distribution (OOD) evaluations, our LTR-enhanced model consistently demonstrates substantially stronger generalisation capabilities compared to standard cross-entropy approaches. However, qualitative and quantitative analyses underline that current LTR optimisations cannot fully overcome representational bottlenecks, resulting in catastrophic predictive breakdown for rare `Tail' classes under severe domain shift. For maximum reproducibility, all dataset splits, key code, and network weights are published with this paper at https://github.com/ZehuaLiuY/Species-Classification.

</details>


### [173] [When 2D Cues Fail: Improving Image Manipulation Localization with Reliable 3D Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18040)
*Guofeng Yu,Zhiqing Guo,Dan Ma,Gaobo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出几何感知框架GFrame，通过单目重建获取深度和表面法向量的可靠部分作为几何线索，与RGB特征融合以提升图像操作定位性能，尤其在操作区域与背景深度融合的情况下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像操作定位方法依赖2D取证线索（如低级伪影、噪声痕迹、语义不一致），但当操作区域与周围背景外观深度融合时，这些线索判别力显著下降。操作区域虽保持局部外观一致，却可能违反场景几何结构。因此需要引入几何推理以超越纯2D证据。

Method: 利用单目重建获取深度和表面法向量作为辅助几何线索；估计几何线索的可靠性并选择性利用，而非直接使用有噪声的重建结果；设计几何感知框架GFrame，将可靠几何特征与RGB特征融合，并跨尺度传播以改进细粒度定位。

Result: 在有限预算约束下，该方法实现了优异的定位性能，表明可靠的3D几何信息能为IML提供超越传统2D线索的互补取证证据。

Conclusion: 可靠的3D几何线索是对传统2D取证线索的有效补充，能够在操作区域深度融合的场景下显著提升定位能力。

Abstract: Existing image manipulation localization (IML) methods rely heavily on 2D forensic cues, such as low-level artifacts, noise traces, and semantic inconsistencies in the manipulated image. While effective in many cases, these cues become much less discriminative when manipulated regions are well blended with their surrounding context in appearance. In such cases, a manipulated region may remain locally appearance-consistent, but still violate the geometric structure of the surrounding scene. This limitation motivates us to go beyond purely 2D evidence and introduce geometric reasoning into IML. To this end, we leverage monocular reconstruction to obtain auxiliary geometric cues, including depth and surface normals. However, a key challenge lies in the fact that reconstructed geometry on manipulated images is inherently noisy and cannot be used naively. Rather than treating depth and normals as direct evidence, we estimate their reliability and exploit them selectively for localization. Based on this principle, we design a geometry-aware framework (GFrame) that fuses reliable geometric cues with RGB features and propagates them across scales to improve fine-grained localization. Extensive experiments show that the proposed method achieves excellent performance under limited budget constraints. These results indicate that reliable 3D geometry provides complementary forensic evidence beyond traditional 2D cues for IML. Related code will be released.

</details>


### [174] [Anticipate Before Acting: Future-State-Conditioned Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18042)
*Lingfeng Zhang,Zhanguang Zhang,Liheng Ma,Tongtong Cao,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FSC-VLN方法，通过在训练时引入未来视觉信息作为监督（未来查询token与Δ步后的视觉嵌入对齐），在不依赖测试时未来图像的情况下提升视觉语言导航性能，在R2R验证集上相比基线方法有显著提升，尤其在长程任务上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准行为克隆仅监督下一步动作，未显式训练策略状态对未来视觉结果进行预测；作者先通过诊断实验确认未来图像（特权信息）对当前动作选择有用，进而探索如何在测试时不依赖未来图像的情况下利用未来信息。

Method: 提出Future-State-Conditioned VLN（FSC-VLN），在模型中添加未来查询token，通过训练阶段的目标分支将其隐藏状态与Δ步后的冻结视觉嵌入对齐；该目标分支在训练后移除，测试时不需未来图像。采用双查询设计，分别处理未来查询和动作查询。

Result: 在R2R val-unseen上，FSC-VLN在两种训练数据规模下均提升了SR/OSR/SPL指标，长程任务增益更大；消融实验验证了双查询设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过训练时对齐未来视觉表征，可以在不牺牲测试时部署可行性的前提下提升VLN策略的导航性能，尤其对长程导航任务效果显著。

Abstract: End-to-end vision-language navigation (VLN) with causal vision-language models can map instructions and egocentric observations directly to actions, but standard behavior cloning supervises only the next action and does not explicitly train the policy state to be predictive of future visual outcomes. We first ask a diagnostic question: if the policy is given an expert-trajectory future image as privileged input at training and testing time, is that additional visual evidence useful for choosing the current action? (These expert-trajectory future images are unavailable at test time in real deployment, so we use this setting only as a privileged-input diagnostic.) The answer is yes; this sanity check shows that future observations can provide rich, actionable cues.
  We then ask a deployable question: without accessing future images at inference, can we still benefit from future information by using a compressed future visual latent only as training supervision? We propose Future-State-Conditioned VLN (FSC-VLN), which adds a future-query token and aligns its hidden state to a frozen visual embedding $Δ$ steps ahead via a training-only target branch that is removed after training. On R2R val-unseen, FSC-VLN improves SR/OSR/SPL over a StreamVLN-style baseline under two training-data regimes, with larger gains on long-horizon episodes; ablations further support the dual-query design (separating future and action queries).

</details>


### [175] [SAR Vessel Detection and Gross Tonnage Estimation from Heterogeneous Datasets for Dark Vessel Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18051)
*Davide Paltrinieri,Andrea Diecidue,Roberto Basla,Daniele Casciani,Piero Fraternali,Giacomo Boracchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合多任务深度学习和非参数模型的框架，利用异构图像和表格数据预测船只位置、类型、物理尺寸及总吨位，进而识别关闭应答器的“暗船”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测非法活动的暗船（关闭应答器的船只）对海上安全至关重要，但现有深度学习模型无法仅基于SAR图像区分哪些船只需携带应答器（取决于总吨位），且缺乏用于端到端训练的公开SAR数据集。

Method: 采用多任务深度学习框架同时预测船只位置、类型和物理尺寸，再级联一个基于KNN的非参数模型，通过混合欧氏距离与分类距离度量样本相似性，从船只尺寸和类别回归总吨位。

Result: 该框架能同时预测多个输出，在各子任务上保持与现有最优模型相当的竞争力，从而有效实现暗船识别。

Conclusion: 提出了一种利用异构数据（图像+表格）的框架，解决了SAR图像中船只检测与总吨位回归的联合任务，为暗船检测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Detecting vessels engaging in illegal activities is of paramount importance for maritime security. One of the major goals is to detect dark vessels, ships that disable their transponders to evade surveillance. Deep Learning (DL) models can detect vessels in Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) images, enabling maritime traffic analysis regardless of weather or visibility conditions. However, to detect potential dark vessels, a DL model must select only those that are required to carry a transponder based on their Gross Tonnage (GT). Unfortunately, no public SAR dataset is available for training an end-to-end DL model for vessel detection and GT regression. In this work, we present a framework that leverages heterogeneous image and tabular datasets to solve this task. Our solution combines a multi-task DL framework for predicting the location, vessel type, and physical dimensions of ships, cascaded with a non-parametric model for predicting GT from vessel size and category. We perform GT regression by a KNN that measures sample similarity using a hybrid Euclidean and categorical distance. Experiments show that our solution can predict multiple outputs while remaining competitive with state-of-the-art models on individual subtasks, thus enabling the identification of dark vessels. We publish our code on GitHub https://github.com/PaltrinieriDavide/vesseldetection.

</details>


### [176] [QIRF Quantum-Inspired Non-Orthogonal Function-Space Compression for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18067)
*Shizeng Jiang,Hao Zhang,Xuerui Ma,Ying Hu,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QIRF是一种受量子启发的非正交函数空间压缩方法，通过构建局部非正交基、重叠矩阵和辐射-响应密度矩阵，利用广义特征分解识别主导子空间并选择代表性高斯基元，在Mip-NeRF 360等13个场景中平均减少71.7%的高斯数量和PLY存储（约3.54倍压缩），同时保持甚至略微提升重建质量（PSNR+0.10 dB），渲染速度提升34.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS压缩方法主要通过基元剪枝、属性量化、聚类或神经编码来减少冗余，但由强重叠且非正交的高斯基函数导致的冗余尚未被充分探索。

Method: 将相邻高斯基元建模为局部非正交基，构造解析高斯重叠矩阵和辐射-响应密度矩阵，通过广义特征分解识别主导局部子空间并选择代表性高斯基元，结合RRDM响应模型和细节感知保护机制在激进剪枝下保留高频结构。

Result: 在13个场景上平均减少71.7%的高斯数量和原始PLY存储（约3.54倍压缩），PSNR平均提升0.10 dB，渲染速度提升34.3%。
